   126	
   127	function inferFollowUpReviewScore(output: { findings: ReviewFinding[]; furtherPassesLowYield: boolean }): 1 | 2 | 3 {
   128	  const severities = new Set(output.findings.map((finding) => finding.severity));
   129	  if (severities.has('critical') || severities.has('high')) {
   130	    return 3;
   131	  }
   132	  if (severities.has('medium')) {
   133	    return 2;
   134	  }
   135	  if (output.findings.length === 0) {
   136	    return output.furtherPassesLowYield ? 1 : 2;
   137	  }
   138	  return 1;
   139	}
   140	
   141	function deriveFollowUpReviewMetadata(
   142	  payload: unknown,
   143	  output: { findings: ReviewFinding[]; furtherPassesLowYield: boolean }
   144	): { score: 1 | 2 | 3; rationale: string } {
   145	  const record = asRecord(payload);
   146	  const rawScore = typeof record.followUpReviewScore === 'number' ? Math.floor(record.followUpReviewScore) : null;
   147	  const score = rawScore === 1 || rawScore === 2 || rawScore === 3 ? rawScore : inferFollowUpReviewScore(output);
   148	  const rationaleCandidate = typeof record.followUpReviewRationale === 'string' ? record.followUpReviewRationale.trim() : '';
   149	  const rationale =
   150	    rationaleCandidate ||
   151	    (score === 3
   152	      ? 'High-severity findings remain in changed paths; another review pass is required after fixes.'
   153	      : score === 2
   154	        ? 'At least one non-trivial issue remains; a follow-up review pass is recommended after fixes.'
   155	        : 'Findings are low severity or low signal; additional review passes are likely diminishing returns.');
   156	
   157	  return { score, rationale };
   158	}

   218	function normalizePath(value: unknown): string | null {
   219	  if (typeof value !== 'string') {

   225	
   226	function recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(
   227	  state: ReviewEvidenceState,
   228	  action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }>,
   229	  toolOutput: unknown,
   230	  changedPaths: Set<string>
   231	): void {
   232	  if (action.tool === 'diff_summary') {
   233	    state.diffSummaryUsed = true;
   234	    return;
   235	  }
   236	
   237	  if (action.tool === 'read_file') {
   238	    const readPath = normalizePath(action.args.path);
   239	    if (readPath) {
   240	      if (changedPaths.has(readPath)) {
   241	        state.readChangedPaths.add(readPath);
   242	      } else {
   243	        state.readCrossFilePaths.add(readPath);
   244	      }
   245	    }
   246	    return;
   247	  }
   248	
   249	  if (action.tool === 'read_batch') {
   250	    const files = Array.isArray(asRecord(toolOutput).files) ? (asRecord(toolOutput).files as unknown[]) : [];
   251	    for (const file of files) {
   252	      const readPath = normalizePath(asRecord(file).path);
   253	      if (!readPath) {
   254	        continue;
   255	      }
   256	      if (changedPaths.has(readPath)) {
   257	        state.readChangedPaths.add(readPath);
   258	      } else {
   259	        state.readCrossFilePaths.add(readPath);
   260	      }
   260	      }
   261	    }
   262	    return;
   263	  }
   264	
   265	  if (action.tool === 'search_code') {
   266	    state.searchUsed = true;
   267	    const matches = Array.isArray(asRecord(toolOutput).matches) ? (asRecord(toolOutput).matches as unknown[]) : [];
   268	    state.searchMatchedChangedPath = matches.some((match) => {
   269	      const matchPath = normalizePath(asRecord(match).path);
   270	      return Boolean(matchPath && changedPaths.has(matchPath));
   271	    });
   272	    state.searchMatchedCrossFilePath = matches.some((match) => {
   273	      const matchPath = normalizePath(asRecord(match).path);
   274	      return Boolean(matchPath && !changedPaths.has(matchPath));
   275	    });
   276	  }
   277	}

   279	function collectMissingEvidenceRequirements(input: {
   280	  evidence: ReviewEvidenceState;
   281	  changedPaths: string[];
   282	}): string[] {
   283	  const missing: string[] = [];
   284	  const sensitiveChangedPaths = input.changedPaths.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));
   285	
   286	  if (input.changedPaths.length > 0 && !input.evidence.diffSummaryUsed) {
   287	    missing.push('Run diff_summary at least once before completing analysis.');
   288	  }
   289	
   290	  const requiredReadCoverage =
   291	    input.changedPaths.length <= MAX_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE_REQUIREMENT
   292	      ? input.changedPaths.length
   293	      : Math.min(MIN_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE, input.changedPaths.length);
   294	  if (input.evidence.readChangedPaths.size < requiredReadCoverage) {
   295	    missing.push(
   296	      `Read at least ${requiredReadCoverage} changed file(s) directly with read_file (currently ${input.evidence.readChangedPaths.size}).`
   297	    );
   298	  }
   299	
   300	  const missingSensitivePaths = sensitiveChangedPaths.filter((path) => !input.evidence.readChangedPaths.has(path));
   301	  if (missingSensitivePaths.length > 0) {
   302	    missing.push(`Read sensitive changed file(s) directly: ${missingSensitivePaths.join(', ')}.`);
   303	  }
   304	
   305	  if (input.evidence.searchMatchedCrossFilePath && input.evidence.readCrossFilePaths.size === 0) {
   306	    missing.push('Read at least one non-changed file that defines or handles an integration boundary touched by the diff.');
   307	  }
   308	
   309	  if (input.changedPaths.length > 0 && !input.evidence.searchUsed) {
   310	    missing.push('Run search_code at least once before completing analysis.');
   311	  }
   312	
   313	  return missing;
   314	}

   316	function selectDeterministicReadPaths(changedPaths: string[]): string[] {
   317	  const deduped = Array.from(new Set(changedPaths));
   318	  const sensitive = deduped.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));
   319	  const requiredCount =
   320	    deduped.length <= MAX_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE_REQUIREMENT
   321	      ? deduped.length
   322	      : Math.min(MIN_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE, deduped.length);
   323	  const selected = [...sensitive];
   324	  for (const path of deduped) {
   325	    if (selected.length >= Math.max(requiredCount, sensitive.length)) {
   326	      break;
   327	    }
   328	    if (!selected.includes(path)) {
   329	      selected.push(path);
   330	    }
   331	  }
   332	  return selected.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_READ_PATHS);
   333	}
   334	
   335	function pathStem(path: string): string | null {
   336	  const fileName = path.split('/').pop() ?? '';
   337	  const stem = fileName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '').trim();
   338	  if (stem.length < DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_MIN_QUERY_LENGTH) {
   339	    return null;
   340	  }
   341	  return stem;
   342	}
   343	
   344	function maybeAddSearchQuery(queries: string[], seen: Set<string>, candidate: string): void {
   345	  const normalized = candidate.trim();
   346	  if (normalized.length < DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_MIN_QUERY_LENGTH) {
   347	    return;
   348	  }
   349	  if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(normalized)) {
   350	    return;
   351	  }
   352	  const lowered = normalized.toLowerCase();
   353	  if (IGNORABLE_SEARCH_TOKENS.has(lowered)) {
   354	    return;
   355	  }
   356	  if (seen.has(lowered)) {
   357	    return;
   358	  }
   359	  seen.add(lowered);
   360	  queries.push(normalized);
   361	}

   363	type DeterministicSearchKind = 'api_route' | 'imported_symbol' | 'callback' | 'dependency';
   364	
   365	interface DeterministicSearchQuery {
   366	  query: string;
   367	  sourcePath: string;
   368	  kind: DeterministicSearchKind;
   369	}
   370	
   371	function maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(
   372	  queries: DeterministicSearchQuery[],
   373	  seen: Set<string>,
   374	  candidate: string,
   375	  sourcePath: string,
   376	  kind: DeterministicSearchKind
   377	): void {
   378	  const tempQueries: string[] = [];
   379	  const beforeSize = seen.size;
   380	  maybeAddSearchQuery(tempQueries, seen, candidate);
   381	  if (seen.size > beforeSize && tempQueries.length > 0) {
   382	    queries.push({ query: tempQueries[0], sourcePath, kind });
   383	  }
   384	}
   385	
   386	function extractDeterministicSearchQueries(changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   387	  const queries: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   388	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   389	  const sensitiveFiles = changedFiles.filter((file) => isSensitiveChangedPath(file.path));
   390	  for (const file of sensitiveFiles) {
   391	    const importMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\bimport\s+(?:type\s+)?(?:\{([^}]+)\}|([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*))/g);
   392	    for (const match of importMatches) {
   393	      const importedBlock = match[1] ?? match[2] ?? '';
   394	      for (const item of importedBlock.split(',')) {
   395	        const cleaned = item.replace(/\bas\b.+$/i, '').trim();
   396	        maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, cleaned, file.path, 'dependency');
   397	        if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   398	          return queries;
   399	        }
   400	      }
   401	    }
   402	
   403	    const callMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\b([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{3,})\s*\(/g);
   404	    for (const match of callMatches) {
   405	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, match[1] ?? '', file.path, 'dependency');
   406	      if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   407	        return queries;
   408	      }
   409	    }
   410	
   411	    const stem = pathStem(file.path);
   412	    if (stem) {
   413	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, stem, file.path, 'dependency');
   414	      if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   415	        return queries;
   416	      }
   417	    }
   418	  }
   419	
   420	  if (queries.length === 0) {
   421	    const fallbackStem = changedFiles.map((file) => pathStem(file.path)).find(Boolean);
   422	    if (fallbackStem) {
   423	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, fallbackStem, changedFiles[0]?.path ?? '.', 'dependency');
   424	    }
   425	  }
   426	
   427	  return queries.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES);
   428	}
   429	
   430	export function extractDeterministicSearchQueriesForTests(
   431	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']
   432	): string[] {
   433	  return extractDeterministicSearchQueries(changedFiles).map((entry) => entry.query);
   434	}
   435	
   436	function extractStaticApiTailQueries(content: string): string[] {
   437	  const queries: string[] = [];
   438	  const lines = content.split('\n');
   439	  for (const line of lines) {
   440	    if (!line.includes('/api/')) {
   441	      continue;
   442	    }
   443	    const segments = Array.from(line.matchAll(/\/([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{3,})(?=(?:\/|\$|['"`)}\],; ]))/g))
   444	      .map((match) => match[1] ?? '')
   445	      .filter(Boolean);
   446	    for (let index = segments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
   447	      const segment = segments[index];
   448	      if (segment === 'api' || segment === 'review' || segment === 'reviews' || segment === 'workspace' || segment === 'workspaces') {
   449	        continue;
   450	      }
   451	      queries.push(`/${segment}`);
   452	      break;
   453	    }
   454	  }
   455	  return queries;
   456	}
   457	
   458	function extractIntegrationSearchQueries(changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   459	  const queries: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   460	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   461	
   462	  for (const file of changedFiles) {
   463	    for (const query of extractStaticApiTailQueries(file.content)) {
   464	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, query, file.path, 'api_route');
   465	      if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   466	        return queries;
   467	      }
   468	    }
   469	
   470	    const importedNames = new Set<string>();
   471	    const importMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\bimport\s+(?:type\s+)?(?:\{([^}]+)\}|([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*))/g);
   472	    for (const match of importMatches) {
   473	      const importedBlock = match[1] ?? match[2] ?? '';
   474	      for (const item of importedBlock.split(',')) {
   475	        const cleaned = item.replace(/\bas\b.+$/i, '').trim();
   476	        if (!cleaned || !INTEGRATION_SYMBOL_KEYWORDS.test(cleaned)) {
   477	          continue;
   478	        }
   479	        importedNames.add(cleaned);
   480	      }
   481	    }
   482	    let importedQueriesAdded = 0;
   483	    for (const importedName of importedNames) {
   484	      if (!new RegExp(`\\b${importedName}\\s*\\(`).test(file.content)) {
   485	        continue;
   486	      }
   487	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, importedName, file.path, 'imported_symbol');
   488	      importedQueriesAdded += 1;
   489	      if (
   490	        queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES ||
   491	        importedQueriesAdded >= MAX_INTEGRATION_IMPORTED_SYMBOL_QUERIES_PER_FILE
   492	      ) {
   493	        break;
   494	      }
   495	    }
   496	    if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   497	      return queries;
   498	    }
   499	
   500	    let callbackQueriesAdded = 0;
   501	    const callbackMatches = file.content.matchAll(/\bon[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9_]{3,}\b/g);
   502	    for (const match of callbackMatches) {
   503	      const callbackName = match[0] ?? '';
   504	      if (!INTEGRATION_CALLBACK_PATTERN.test(callbackName)) {
   505	        continue;
   506	      }
   507	      maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(queries, seen, callbackName, file.path, 'callback');
   508	      callbackQueriesAdded += 1;
   509	      if (
   510	        queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES ||
   511	        callbackQueriesAdded >= MAX_INTEGRATION_CALLBACK_QUERIES_PER_FILE
   512	      ) {
   513	        break;
   514	      }
   515	    }
   516	    if (queries.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   517	      return queries;
   518	    }
   519	  }
   520	
   520	
   521	  return queries.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES);
   522	}
   523	
   524	export function extractIntegrationSearchQueriesForTests(
   525	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles']
   526	): string[] {
   527	  return extractIntegrationSearchQueries(changedFiles).map((entry) => entry.query);
   528	}

   556	function scoreSearchMatch(input: {
   557	  sourcePath: string;
   558	  candidatePath: string;
   559	  kind: DeterministicSearchKind;
   560	  query: string;
   561	}): number {
   562	  let score = pathDistanceScore(input.sourcePath, input.candidatePath);
   563	  if (input.kind === 'api_route') {
   564	    if (/\/src\/api\//.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   565	      score -= 6;
   566	    }
   567	    if (/packages\/worker\//.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   568	      score -= 3;
   569	    }
   570	    if (/recover|fail|retry|events/i.test(input.query) && /recover|fail|retry|events/i.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   571	      score -= 3;
   572	    }
   573	    if (/reviews/i.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   574	      score -= 1;
   575	    }
   576	    return score;
   577	  }
   578	
   579	  if (input.kind === 'imported_symbol' || input.kind === 'callback') {
   580	    if (topLevelPackage(input.candidatePath) === topLevelPackage(input.sourcePath)) {
   581	      score -= 4;
   582	    }
   583	    if (input.candidatePath.split('/').slice(0, 3).join('/') === input.sourcePath.split('/').slice(0, 3).join('/')) {
   584	      score -= 2;
   585	    }
   586	  }
   587	
   588	  return score;
   589	}
   590	
   591	function combineDeterministicSearchQueries(
   592	  primary: DeterministicSearchQuery[],
   593	  secondary: DeterministicSearchQuery[]
   594	): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   595	  const combined: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   596	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   597	  for (const query of [...primary, ...secondary]) {
   598	    const lowered = query.query.trim().toLowerCase();
   599	    if (!lowered || seen.has(lowered)) {
   600	      continue;
   601	    }
   602	    seen.add(lowered);
   603	    combined.push(query);
   604	    if (combined.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   605	      break;
   606	    }
   607	  }
   608	  return combined;
   609	}
   610	
   611	function pathDistanceScore(sourcePath: string, candidatePath: string): number {
   612	  const sourceSegments = sourcePath.split('/');
   613	  const candidateSegments = candidatePath.split('/');
   614	  let shared = 0;
   615	  while (
   616	    shared < sourceSegments.length &&
   617	    shared < candidateSegments.length &&
   618	    sourceSegments[shared] === candidateSegments[shared]
   619	  ) {
   620	    shared += 1;
   621	  }
   622	  return (sourceSegments.length - shared) + (candidateSegments.length - shared);
   623	}
   624	
   625	export function computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(maxSteps: number): { deterministicMaxSteps: number; providerMaxSteps: number } {
   626	  const providerMaxSteps = Math.max(1, maxSteps - Math.max(0, maxSteps - MIN_PROVIDER_REASONING_STEPS));
   627	  const deterministicMaxSteps = Math.max(0, maxSteps - providerMaxSteps);
   628	  return { deterministicMaxSteps, providerMaxSteps };
   629	}
   630	
   631	async function runDeterministicEvidenceCollection(input: {
   632	  sandbox: SandboxClient;
   633	  policy: ReviewCommandPolicy;
   634	  maxFileBytes: number;
   635	  authoritativeDiffSnapshot: unknown;
   636	  changedPaths: string[];
   637	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles'];
   638	  changedPathsSet: Set<string>;
   639	  evidence: ReviewEvidenceState;
   640	  usedTools: string[];
   641	  history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[];
   642	  maxDeterministicSteps: number;
   643	  onLifecycleEvent?: (eventType: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) => void | Promise<void>;
   644	}): Promise<number> {
   645	  const deterministicTools: Array<Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }>> = [
   646	    { type: 'tool', tool: 'diff_summary', args: { maxBytes: 64_000 } },
   647	    ...selectDeterministicReadPaths(input.changedPaths).map((path) => ({
   648	      type: 'tool' as const,
   649	      tool: 'read_file' as const,
   650	      args: { path, maxBytes: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 48_000) },
   651	    })),
   652	  ];
   653	  const followUpReadPaths: string[] = [];
   654	
   655	  let deterministicStep = 0;
   656	  for (const action of deterministicTools) {
   657	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   658	      break;
   659	    }
   660	    deterministicStep += 1;
   661	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   662	      input.sandbox,
   663	      action,
   664	      input.policy,
   665	      input.maxFileBytes,
   666	      action.tool === 'diff_summary' ? input.authoritativeDiffSnapshot : undefined
   667	    );
   668	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   669	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   670	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   671	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   672	        step: deterministicStep,
   673	        tool: action.tool,
   674	        deterministic: true,
   675	      });
   676	    }
   677	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   678	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   679	  }
   680	
   681	  const deterministicSearchQueries = combineDeterministicSearchQueries(
   682	    extractIntegrationSearchQueries(input.changedFiles),
   683	    extractDeterministicSearchQueries(input.changedFiles)
   684	  );
   685	  for (const searchQuery of deterministicSearchQueries) {
   686	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   687	      break;
   688	    }
   689	    const action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }> = {
   690	      type: 'tool',
   691	      tool: 'search_code',
   692	      args: {
   693	        query: searchQuery.query,
   694	        path: '.',
   695	        maxResults: 20,
   696	        maxBytesPerFile: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 16_000),
   697	      },
   698	    };
   699	    deterministicStep += 1;
   700	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   701	      input.sandbox,
   702	      action,
   703	      input.policy,
   704	      input.maxFileBytes,
   705	    );
   706	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   707	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   708	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   709	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   710	        step: deterministicStep,
   711	        tool: action.tool,
   712	        deterministic: true,
   713	        query: searchQuery.query,
   714	      });
   715	    }
   716	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   717	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   718	
   719	    const matches = Array.isArray(asRecord(output.result).matches) ? (asRecord(output.result).matches as unknown[]) : [];
   720	    const rankedMatches = matches
   721	      .map((match) => {
   722	        const matchPath = normalizePath(asRecord(match).path);
   723	        return matchPath
   724	          ? {
   725	              matchPath,
   726	              score: scoreSearchMatch({
   727	                sourcePath: searchQuery.sourcePath,
   728	                candidatePath: matchPath,
   729	                kind: searchQuery.kind,
   730	                query: searchQuery.query,
   731	              }),
   732	            }
   733	          : null;
   734	      })
   735	      .filter((match): match is { matchPath: string; score: number } => Boolean(match))
   736	      .sort((left, right) => left.score - right.score);
   737	    for (const match of rankedMatches) {
   738	      const matchPath = match.matchPath;
   739	      if (input.changedPathsSet.has(matchPath) || followUpReadPaths.includes(matchPath)) {
   740	        continue;
   741	      }
   742	      followUpReadPaths.push(matchPath);
   743	      if (followUpReadPaths.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_CROSS_FILE_READS) {
   744	        break;
   745	      }
   746	    }
   747	  }
   748	
   749	  for (const path of followUpReadPaths) {
   750	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   751	      break;
   752	    }
   753	    const batchPaths = [path, ...followUpReadPaths.filter((candidate) => candidate !== path)].slice(0, 2);
   754	    const action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }> = {
   755	      type: 'tool',
   756	      tool: 'read_batch',
   757	      args: { paths: batchPaths, maxBytes: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 32_000) },
   758	    };
   759	    deterministicStep += 1;
   760	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   761	      input.sandbox,
   762	      action,
   763	      input.policy,
   764	      input.maxFileBytes,
   765	    );
   766	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   767	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   768	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   769	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   770	        step: deterministicStep,
   771	        tool: action.tool,
   772	        deterministic: true,
   773	        crossFile: true,
   774	      });
   775	    }
   776	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   777	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   778	    break;
   779	  }
   780	
   781	  return deterministicStep;
   782	}
   783	
   784	/**
   785	 * Hydrates a temporary sandbox from a stored source bundle and reads a bounded set of files
   786	 * through the same read-only tool path used by review analysis.
   787	 */
   788	export async function readWorkspaceFilesFromSourceBundle(
   789	  env: Env,
   790	  input: {
   791	    sourceBundleKey: string;
   792	    sandboxId: string;
   793	    paths: string[];
   794	    maxFileBytes?: number;
   795	  }
   796	): Promise<ReviewSourceFileReadResult[]> {
   797	  const maxFileBytes = parseIntegerString(env.REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_FILE_BYTES, DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_FILE_BYTES, 1_024, 200_000);
   798	  const effectiveMaxFileBytes =
   799	    typeof input.maxFileBytes === 'number' && Number.isFinite(input.maxFileBytes)
   800	      ? Math.max(1_024, Math.min(maxFileBytes, Math.floor(input.maxFileBytes)))
   801	      : maxFileBytes;
   802	
   803	  if (!env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS && !env.SOURCE_BUNDLES) {
   804	    throw new Error('WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS or SOURCE_BUNDLES binding is required for review context assembly');
   805	  }
   806	  const bundle =
   807	    (env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS ? await env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null) ??
   808	    (env.SOURCE_BUNDLES ? await env.SOURCE_BUNDLES.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null);
   809	  if (!bundle) {
   810	    throw new Error(`Review source bundle not found: ${input.sourceBundleKey}`);
   811	  }
   812	
   813	  const sandbox = await resolveReviewSandbox(env, input.sandboxId);
   814	  try {
   815	    await hydrateReviewSandbox(sandbox, await bundle.arrayBuffer());
   816	
   817	    const policy: ReviewCommandPolicy = {
   818	      commandAllow: [],
   819	      commandDeny: [],
   820	      maxCommandTimeoutMs: MAX_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_MS,
   821	      maxOutputBytes: DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES,
   822	      rootPath: WORKSPACE_ROOT,
   823	    };
   824	
   825	    const uniquePaths = Array.from(new Set(input.paths.map((path) => path.trim()).filter(Boolean)));
   826	    const results: ReviewSourceFileReadResult[] = [];
   827	    for (const path of uniquePaths) {
   828	      const toolResult = await executeReviewTool(
   829	        sandbox,
   830	        { type: 'tool', tool: 'read_file', args: { path, maxBytes: effectiveMaxFileBytes } },
   831	        policy,
   832	        effectiveMaxFileBytes
   833	      );
   834	      const resultRecord = asRecord(toolResult.result);
   835	      const error = typeof resultRecord.error === 'string' ? resultRecord.error : null;
   836	      const content = typeof resultRecord.content === 'string' ? resultRecord.content : null;
   837	      const bytes = typeof resultRecord.bytes === 'number' && Number.isFinite(resultRecord.bytes)
   838	        ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(resultRecord.bytes))
   839	        : 0;
   840	      const truncated = Boolean(resultRecord.truncated);
   841	      results.push({ path, content, bytes, truncated, error });
   842	    }
   843	
   844	    return results;
   845	  } finally {
   846	    if (typeof sandbox.destroy === 'function') {
   847	      try {
   848	        await sandbox.destroy();
   849	      } catch {
   850	        // Best-effort cleanup only.
   851	      }
   852	    }
   853	  }
   854	}
   855	
   856	/**
   857	 * Runs the model-backed review analysis loop against a hydrated deployment snapshot using
   858	 * the read-only review tools and strict structured-output validation.
   859	 */
   860	export async function runWorkspaceDeploymentAgentAnalysis(
   861	  env: Env,
   862	  input: ReviewAgentPromptInput & {
   863	    deploymentSandboxId: string;
   864	    modelOverride?: string;
   865	  }
   866	): Promise<ReviewAgentAnalysisResult | null> {
   867	  const endpoint = (env.AGENT_SDK_URL ?? '').trim();
   868	  const openrouterApiKey=[REDACTED](input.openrouterApiKey) ?? readOptionalString(env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY);
   869	  if (!endpoint && !openrouterApiKey) {
   870	    return null;
   871	  }
   872	
   873	  const model =
   874	    readOptionalString(input.modelOverride) ??
   875	    readOptionalString(env.REVIEW_MODEL) ??
   876	    readOptionalString(env.AGENT_MODEL) ??
   877	    DEFAULT_REVIEW_MODEL;
   878	  const reasoningEffort = parseReviewReasoningEffort(readOptionalString(env.REVIEW_REASONING_EFFORT));
   879	  const authToken = (env.AGENT_SDK_AUTH_TOKEN ?? '').trim() || null;
   880	  let endpointHost: string | null = null;
   881	  let endpointPath: string | null = null;
   882	  try {
   883	    const parsedEndpoint = new URL(endpoint);
   884	    endpointHost = parsedEndpoint.host;
   885	    endpointPath = parsedEndpoint.pathname;
   886	  } catch {
   887	    endpointHost = null;
   888	    endpointPath = null;
   889	  }
   890	
   891	  const maxSteps = parseIntegerString(env.REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_STEPS, DEFAULT_REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_STEPS, 1, MAX_REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_STEPS);
   892	  const maxFileBytes = parseIntegerString(env.REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_FILE_BYTES, DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_FILE_BYTES, 1_024, 200_000);
   893	  if (!env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS && !env.SOURCE_BUNDLES) {
   894	    throw new Error('WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS or SOURCE_BUNDLES binding is required for review analysis');
   895	  }
   896	  const bundle =
   897	    (env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS ? await env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null) ??
   898	    (env.SOURCE_BUNDLES ? await env.SOURCE_BUNDLES.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null);
   899	  if (!bundle) {
   900	    throw new Error(`Review source bundle not found: ${input.sourceBundleKey}`);
   900	    throw new Error(`Review source bundle not found: ${input.sourceBundleKey}`);
   901	  }
   902	
   903	  const sandbox = await resolveReviewSandbox(env, input.deploymentSandboxId);
   904	  try {
   905	    await hydrateReviewSandbox(sandbox, await bundle.arrayBuffer());
   906	    const { rootListing, diffSnapshot } = await snapshotInitialContext(sandbox, maxFileBytes);
   907	    const prompt = buildReviewAgentPrompt(
   908	      sanitizePromptInput({
   909	        ...input,
   910	        rootListing,
   911	        diffSnapshot,
   912	      })
   913	    );
   914	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   915	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_prompt_built', {
   916	        reviewContextId: input.reviewContext.id,
   917	        reasoningEffort: reasoningEffort ?? null,
   918	      });
   919	    }
   920	
   921	    const provider = openrouterApiKey
   922	      ? new OpenRouterReviewProvider(openrouterApiKey, validateReviewAgentAction, 'https://nimbus.dayhaysoos.com', 'Nimbus Review Harness')
   923	      : new CloudflareAgentSdkReviewProvider(
   924	          endpoint,
   925	          authToken,
   926	          env.AGENT_ENDPOINT ?? null,
   927	          readOptionalString(input.openrouterApiKey),
   928	          validateReviewAgentAction
   929	        );
   930	    const providerName = openrouterApiKey ? 'openrouter' : 'cloudflare_agents_sdk';
   931	    const policy: ReviewCommandPolicy = {
   932	      commandAllow: [],
   933	      commandDeny: ['git ', 'rm ', 'npm ', 'pnpm ', 'yarn ', 'bun ', 'mkdir ', 'mv ', 'cp ', 'touch '],
   934	      maxCommandTimeoutMs: MAX_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_MS,
   935	      maxOutputBytes: DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES,
   936	      rootPath: WORKSPACE_ROOT,
   937	    };
   938	
   939	    const history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[] = [];
   940	    const usedTools: string[] = [];
   941	    const changedPaths = input.reviewContext.retrieval.changedFiles.map((file) => file.path);
   942	    const changedPathsSet = new Set(changedPaths);
   943	    const integrationSearchQueries = extractIntegrationSearchQueries(input.reviewContext.retrieval.changedFiles);
   944	    const evidence = initializeEvidenceState();
   945	    let finalValidationError: ReviewAgentOutputError | null = null;
   946	    let repairAttempted = false;
   947	    let repairSucceeded = false;
   948	    let firstPassValid = false;
   949	    let validationErrorCount = 0;
   950	    let dedupedExactCount = 0;
   951	    let fallbackApplied = false;
   952	    let fallbackReason: string | null = null;
   953	    let repairAttemptsUsed = 0;
   954	    let forceCompleteNextStep = false;
   955	
   956	    const { deterministicMaxSteps, providerMaxSteps } = computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(maxSteps);
   957	
   958	    const deterministicExecutedSteps = await runDeterministicEvidenceCollection({
   959	      sandbox,
   960	      policy,
   961	      maxFileBytes,
   962	      authoritativeDiffSnapshot: input.authoritativeDiffSnapshot,
   963	      changedPaths,
   964	      changedFiles: input.reviewContext.retrieval.changedFiles,
   965	      changedPathsSet,
   966	      evidence,
   967	      usedTools,
   968	      history,
   969	      maxDeterministicSteps: deterministicMaxSteps,
   970	      onLifecycleEvent: input.onLifecycleEvent,
   971	    });
   972	    const providerLoopMaxSteps = Math.max(providerMaxSteps, maxSteps - deterministicExecutedSteps);
   973	    const sensitiveChangedPaths = changedPaths.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));
   974	    if (sensitiveChangedPaths.length > 0) {
   975	      history.push({
   976	        role: 'assistant',
   977	        content:
   978	          `analysis_focus: prioritize concrete correctness review in sensitive changed paths before lower-risk neighboring code: ${JSON.stringify(sensitiveChangedPaths)}.`,
   979	      });
   980	    }
   981	
   982	    for (let step = 1; step <= providerLoopMaxSteps; step += 1) {
   983	      if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   984	        await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_provider_request_started', {
   985	          step,
   986	          endpointHost,
   987	          endpointPath,
   988	          hasAuthToken: Boolean(authToken),
   989	          hasOpenrouterApiKey: Boolean(openrouterApiKey),
   990	          provider: providerName,
   991	        });
   992	      }
   993	
   994	      const action = await provider.next({
   995	        prompt,
   996	        model,
   997	        reasoningEffort,
   998	        maxSteps: providerLoopMaxSteps,
   999	        step,
  1000	        history,
  1001	        forceComplete: forceCompleteNextStep,
  1002	      });
  1003	      forceCompleteNextStep = false;
  1004	
  1005	      if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
  1006	        await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_step_planned', {
  1007	          step,
  1008	          type: action.type,
  1009	          tool: action.type === 'tool' ? action.tool : null,
  1010	        });
  1011	      }
  1012	
  1013	      if (action.type === 'complete') {
  1014	        const missingEvidence = collectMissingEvidenceRequirements({
  1015	          evidence,
  1016	          changedPaths,
  1017	        });
  1018	        if (missingEvidence.length > 0 && step <= providerLoopMaxSteps) {
  1019	          if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
  1020	            await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_evidence_insufficient', {
  1021	              step,
  1022	              missingEvidence,
  1023	            });
  1024	          }
  1025	          history.push({
  1026	            role: 'assistant',
  1027	            content: 'analysis_guard: completion rejected due to insufficient evidence; gather required tool evidence before completing.',
  1028	          });
  1029	          history.push({
  1030	            role: 'tool',
  1031	            tool: 'analysis_guard',
  1032	            output: {
  1033	              ok: false,
  1034	              missingEvidence,
  1035	              changedFiles: changedPaths,
  1036	            },
  1037	          });
  1038	          continue;
  1039	        }
  1040	
  1041	        if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
  1042	          await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_model_output_received', { step, repairAttempted });
  1043	        }
  1044	        try {
  1045	          const parsed = parseCompleteActionPayload(action);
  1046	          const validated = validateOutputOrThrow(parsed);
  1047	          const followUpReview = deriveFollowUpReviewMetadata(parsed, {
  1048	            findings: validated.output.findings,
  1049	            furtherPassesLowYield: validated.output.furtherPassesLowYield,
  1050	          });
  1051	          firstPassValid = repairAttemptsUsed === 0;
  1052	          repairAttempted = repairAttemptsUsed > 0;
  1053	          repairSucceeded = repairAttemptsUsed > 0;
  1054	          dedupedExactCount = validated.dedupedExactCount;
  1055	          if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
  1056	            await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_output_validated', {
  1057	              firstPassValid,
  1058	              repairAttempted,
  1059	              repairSucceeded,
  1060	              validationErrorCount,
  1061	              findingCount: validated.output.findings.length,
  1062	              followUpReviewScore: followUpReview.score,
  1063	            });
  1064	          }
  1065	          return {
  1066	            findings: validated.output.findings,
  1067	            summary: validated.output.summary,
  1068	            furtherPassesLowYield: validated.output.furtherPassesLowYield,
  1069	            followUpReviewScore: followUpReview.score,
  1070	            followUpReviewRationale: followUpReview.rationale,
  1071	            intent: null,
  1072	            provider: providerName,
  1073	            model,
  1074	            stepsExecuted: step,
  1075	            usedTools,
  1076	            validation: {
  1077	              firstPassValid,
  1078	              repairAttempted,
  1079	              repairSucceeded,
  1080	              validationErrorCount,
  1081	              dedupedExactCount,
  1082	              fallbackApplied,
  1083	              fallbackReason,
  1084	            },
  1085	          };
  1086	        } catch (error) {
  1087	          if (error instanceof ReviewAgentOutputError && action.summary && isGenericProviderCompletionSummary(action.summary)) {
  1088	            error = new ReviewAgentOutputError(
  1089	              'Review agent returned provider completion text instead of structured JSON output'
  1090	            ).withCode('review_analysis_invalid_output', {
  1091	              errors: [{ path: '$', message: 'Provider completion summary returned instead of required JSON payload.' }],
  1092	            });
  1093	          }
  1094	
  1095	          if (error instanceof ReviewAgentOutputError) {

  1097	            const validationErrors = extractValidationErrors(error);
  1098	            validationErrorCount = validationErrors.length;
  1099	            if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {

     1	import type { ReviewFinding } from '../../../types.js';
     2	
     3	export async function replaceReviewFindings(
     4	  db: D1Database,
     5	  reviewId: string,
     6	  findings: ReviewFinding[],
     7	  options?: { startNumber?: number }
     8	): Promise<void> {
     9	  await db.prepare('DELETE FROM review_findings WHERE review_id = ?').bind(reviewId).run();
    10	
    11	  const startNumber = typeof options?.startNumber === 'number' && Number.isFinite(options.startNumber) && options.startNumber > 0
    12	    ? Math.floor(options.startNumber)
    13	    : 1;
    14	
    15	  for (const [index, finding] of findings.entries()) {
    16	    const findingNumber = startNumber + index;
    17	    const findingId = `${reviewId}_F-${String(findingNumber).padStart(3, '0')}`;
    18	    await db
    19	      .prepare(
    20	        `INSERT INTO review_findings (id, review_id, severity, category, pass_type, description, locations_json, suggested_fix)
    21	         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    22	      )
    23	      .bind(
    24	        findingId,
    25	        reviewId,
    26	        finding.severity,
    27	        finding.category,
    28	        finding.passType,
    29	        finding.description,
    30	        JSON.stringify(finding.locations),
    31	        finding.suggestedFix
    32	      )
    33	      .run();
    34	  }
    35	}
    36	
    37	export async function getHighestFindingNumberForBranch(db: D1Database, repo: string, branch: string): Promise<number> {
    38	  const normalizedRepo = repo.trim();
    39	  const normalizedBranch = branch.trim();
    40	  if (!normalizedRepo || !normalizedBranch) {
    41	    return 0;
    42	  }
    43	
    44	  const row = await db
    45	    .prepare(
    46	      `SELECT
    47	         COALESCE(MAX(
    48	           CASE
    49	             WHEN instr(rf.id, '_F-') > 0 THEN CAST(substr(rf.id, instr(rf.id, '_F-') + 3) AS INTEGER)
    50	             ELSE 0
    51	           END
    52	         ), 0) AS max_seq
    53	       FROM review_findings rf
    54	       JOIN review_runs rr ON rr.id = rf.review_id
    55	       WHERE rr.repo = ? AND rr.branch = ?`
    56	    )
    57	    .bind(normalizedRepo, normalizedBranch)
    58	    .first<{ max_seq: number | null }>();
    59	
    60	  const maxSeq = typeof row?.max_seq === 'number' && Number.isFinite(row.max_seq) ? row.max_seq : 0;
    61	  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(maxSeq));
    62	}

+import { strict as assert } from 'assert';
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile } from 'fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'os';
+import { join } from 'path';
+import { execFileSync } from 'child_process';
+import { validateReviewCommitCheckpoint } from '../../../src/commands/review/preflight.js';
+
+function git(cwd: string, args: string[]): string {
+  return execFileSync('git', args, {
+    cwd,
+    encoding: 'utf8',
+    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
+  }).trim();
+}
+
+async function writeAndCommit(
+  repoDir: string,
+  input: {
+    filePath: string;
+    content: string;
+    message: string;
+  }
+): Promise<string> {
+  await writeFile(join(repoDir, input.filePath), input.content, 'utf8');
+  git(repoDir, ['add', input.filePath]);
+  git(repoDir, ['commit', '-m', input.message]);
+  return git(repoDir, ['rev-parse', 'HEAD']);
+}
+
+export async function runReviewPreflightSelectionTests(): Promise<void> {
+  const repoDir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'nimbus-review-preflight-'));
+  try {
+    git(repoDir, ['init', '-b', 'main']);
+    git(repoDir, ['config', 'user.name', 'Nimbus Tests']);
+    git(repoDir, ['config', 'user.email', 'nimbus-tests@example.com']);
+
+    await writeAndCommit(repoDir, {
+      filePath: 'app.txt',
+      content: 'one\n',
+      message: 'feat: checkpoint one\n\nEntire-Checkpoint: aaaaaaaaaaaa',
+    });
+    const secondCheckpointSha = await writeAndCommit(repoDir, {
+      filePath: 'app.txt',
+      content: 'one\ntwo\n',
+      message: 'feat: checkpoint two\n\nEntire-Checkpoint: bbbbbbbbbbbb',
+    });
+    const nonCheckpointHeadSha = await writeAndCommit(repoDir, {
+      filePath: 'app.txt',
+      content: 'one\ntwo\nthree\n',
+      message: 'feat: plain head commit',
+    });
+
+    assert.throws(
+      () => validateReviewCommitCheckpoint('HEAD', repoDir, { lastCheckpoints: 2 }),
+      /This commit has no Entire-Checkpoint trailer\. The last commit on this branch with valid checkpoint context was/
+    );
+
+    const checkpointedHeadSha = await writeAndCommit(repoDir, {
+      filePath: 'app.txt',
+      content: 'one\ntwo\nthree\nfour\n',
+      message: 'feat: checkpoint three\n\nEntire-Checkpoint: cccccccccccc',
+    });
+
+    const resolved = validateReviewCommitCheckpoint('HEAD', repoDir, { lastCheckpoints: 2 });
+    assert.equal(resolved.commitSha, checkpointedHeadSha);
+    assert.equal(resolved.checkpointId, 'cccccccccccc');
+    assert.equal(resolved.checkpointSelectionMode, 'last_n');
+    assert.deepEqual(
+      resolved.includedCheckpoints?.map((checkpoint) => checkpoint.commitSha),
+      [secondCheckpointSha, checkpointedHeadSha]
+    );
+    assert.equal(resolved.commitDiffPatch.includes('three'), true);
+    assert.equal(resolved.commitDiffPatch.includes('four'), true);
+    assert.equal(resolved.commitDiffPatch.includes(nonCheckpointHeadSha.slice(0, 7)), false);
+  } finally {
+    await rm(repoDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+}

    74	function preflightErrorCode(message: string): NonNullable<StudioNewReviewPreflightResult['error']>['code'] {
    75	  const lower = message.toLowerCase();
    76	  if (lower.includes('entire-checkpoint trailer') || lower.includes('no entire session history')) {
    77	    return 'checkpoint_unavailable';
    78	  }
    79	  if (lower.includes('entire session') || lower.includes('checkpoint context')) {
    80	    return 'entire_context_unavailable';
    81	  }
    82	  return 'unknown';
    83	}

    85	function normalizeStudioPolicyMode(value: string | null | undefined): StudioReviewPolicyMode {
    86	  return value === 'review' ? 'review' : 'auto';
    87	}

    89	function resolveStudioRepoRoot(explicitRepoRoot?: string): string {
    90	  if (explicitRepoRoot?.trim()) {
    91	    return explicitRepoRoot.trim();
    92	  }
    93	  try {
    94	    return new GitRepo(process.cwd()).getRepoRoot();
    95	  } catch {
    96	    return process.cwd();
    97	  }
    98	}

   100	function normalizeExpectedContextField(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
   101	  const normalized = value?.trim();
   102	  return normalized ? normalized : null;
   103	}
   104	
   105	export function studioBranchContextMatchesExpected(
   106	  current: { repo: string; branch: string },
   107	  expected?: { repo?: string | null; branch?: string | null }
   108	): boolean {
   109	  const expectedRepo = normalizeExpectedContextField(expected?.repo);
   110	  const expectedBranch = normalizeExpectedContextField(expected?.branch);
   111	  if (!expectedRepo || !expectedBranch) {
   112	    return true;
   113	  }
   114	  return current.repo === expectedRepo && current.branch === expectedBranch;
   115	}

   117	export async function resolveStudioNewReviewPreflight(options?: {
   118	  repoRoot?: string;
   119	  lastCheckpoints?: number;
   120	}): Promise<StudioNewReviewPreflightResult> {
   121	  const repoRoot = resolveStudioRepoRoot(options?.repoRoot);
   122	  const lastCheckpoints = normalizeLastCheckpoints(options?.lastCheckpoints ?? null);
   123	  const preferences = await readStudioPreferences({ repoRoot });
   124	  const policyMode = normalizeStudioPolicyMode(preferences.policyMode);
   125	  let repo: string | null = null;
   126	  let branch: string | null = null;
   127	  try {
   128	    const gitProvenance = resolveReviewGitProvenance(repoRoot);
   129	    repo = gitProvenance.repo;
   130	    branch = gitProvenance.branch;
   131	  } catch {
   132	    repo = null;
   133	    branch = null;
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  let commitSha: string | null = null;
   137	  let checkpointId: string | null = null;
   138	  let includedCheckpoints: Array<{ checkpointId: string; commitSha: string; commitSubject: string }> = [];
   139	  let checkpointSelectionMode: 'latest' | 'last_n' = lastCheckpoints > 1 ? 'last_n' : 'latest';
   140	  let checkpointDetail = '';
   141	  try {
   142	    const checkpoint = validateReviewCommitCheckpoint('HEAD', repoRoot, {
   143	      lastCheckpoints,
   144	    });
   145	    commitSha = checkpoint.commitSha;
   146	    checkpointId = checkpoint.checkpointId;
   147	    includedCheckpoints = checkpoint.includedCheckpoints ?? [];
   148	    checkpointSelectionMode = checkpoint.checkpointSelectionMode === 'last_n' ? 'last_n' : 'latest';
   149	    checkpointDetail =
   150	      checkpointSelectionMode === 'last_n'
   151	        ? `Resolved ${includedCheckpoints.length} checkpoints ending at ${checkpointId}.`
   152	        : `Resolved checkpoint ${checkpointId} from ${commitSha.slice(0, 12)}.`;
   153	  } catch (error) {
   154	    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
   155	    return {
   156	      repo,
   157	      branch,
   158	      policyMode,
   159	      lastCheckpoints,
   160	      checkpointSelectionMode,
   161	      checkpointId: null,
   162	      commitSha: null,
   163	      includedCheckpoints: [],
   164	      ready: false,
   165	      checks: [
   166	        {
   167	          code: 'checkpoint',
   168	          label: 'Checkpoint target',
   169	          ok: false,
   170	          detail: message,
   171	        },
   172	        {
   173	          code: 'entire_context',
   174	          label: 'Entire context',
   175	          ok: false,
   176	          detail: 'Blocked until checkpoint target is available.',
   177	        },
   178	      ],
   179	      error: {
   180	        code: preflightErrorCode(message),
   181	        message,
   182	      },
   183	    };
   184	  }
   185	
   186	  try {
   187	    const context = await validateReviewEntireIntentContext(
   188	      {
   189	        commitSha,
   190	        checkpointId,
   191	      },
   192	      {
   193	        summarizeSession: 'auto',
   194	        allowBranchFallback: true,
   195	      },
   196	      repoRoot
   197	    );
   198	    const contextDetail =
   199	      context.contextResolution === 'branch_fallback'
   200	        ? `Using branch fallback checkpoint ${context.resolvedCheckpointId} from ${context.commitsAgo} commit(s) ago.`
   201	        : 'Readable Entire checkpoint context found for current commit.';
   202	    return {
   203	      repo,
   204	      branch,
   205	      policyMode,
   206	      lastCheckpoints,
   207	      checkpointSelectionMode,
   208	      checkpointId,
   209	      commitSha,
   210	      includedCheckpoints,
   211	      ready: true,
   212	      checks: [
   213	        {
   214	          code: 'checkpoint',
   215	          label: 'Checkpoint target',
   216	          ok: true,
   217	          detail: checkpointDetail,
   218	        },
   219	        {
   220	          code: 'entire_context',
   221	          label: 'Entire context',
   222	          ok: true,
   223	          detail: contextDetail,
   224	        },
   225	      ],
   226	    };
   227	  } catch (error) {
   228	    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
   229	    return {
   230	      repo,
   231	      branch,
   232	      policyMode,
   233	      lastCheckpoints,
   234	      checkpointSelectionMode,
   235	      checkpointId,
   236	      commitSha,
   237	      includedCheckpoints,
   238	      ready: false,
   239	      checks: [
   240	        {
   241	          code: 'checkpoint',
   242	          label: 'Checkpoint target',
   243	          ok: true,
   244	          detail: checkpointDetail,
   245	        },
   246	        {
   247	          code: 'entire_context',
   248	          label: 'Entire context',
   249	          ok: false,
   250	          detail: message,
   251	        },
   252	      ],
   253	      error: {
   254	        code: preflightErrorCode(message),
   255	        message,
   256	      },
   257	    };
   258	  }
   259	}

   261	export async function startStudioNewReview(options: {
   262	  policyMode: StudioReviewPolicyMode;
   263	  lastCheckpoints?: number;
   264	  repoRoot?: string;
   265	  expectedRepo?: string | null;
   266	  expectedBranch?: string | null;
   267	  signal?: AbortSignal;
   268	  onEvent?: (event: StudioNewReviewStartStreamEvent) => void | Promise<void>;
   269	}): Promise<StudioNewReviewStartResult> {
   270	  let abortBeforeReviewCreation = true;
   271	  const throwIfAborted = (): void => {
   272	    if (!abortBeforeReviewCreation || !options.signal?.aborted) {
   273	      return;
   274	    }
   275	    const error = new Error('Studio review start aborted before completion.');
   276	    error.name = 'AbortError';
   277	    throw error;
   278	  };
   279	  const emitEvent = async (event: StudioNewReviewStartStreamEvent): Promise<void> => {
   280	    throwIfAborted();
   281	    try {
   282	      await options.onEvent?.(event);
   283	    } catch {
   284	      // Start-flow events are best-effort only and must not abort review setup.
   285	    }
   286	  };
   287	
   288	  throwIfAborted();
   289	  const policyMode = normalizeStudioPolicyMode(options.policyMode);
   290	  const lastCheckpoints = normalizeLastCheckpoints(options.lastCheckpoints ?? null);
   291	  const repoRoot = resolveStudioRepoRoot(options.repoRoot);
   292	  const workerUrl = getWorkerUrl();
   293	  if (!workerUrl) {
   294	    throw new Error('NIMBUS_WORKER_URL environment variable is required');
   295	  }
   296	
   297	  const gitProvenance = resolveReviewGitProvenance(repoRoot);
   298	  if (
   299	    !studioBranchContextMatchesExpected(gitProvenance, {
   300	      repo: options.expectedRepo,
   301	      branch: options.expectedBranch,
   302	    })
   303	  ) {
   304	    throw new Error(
   305	      `Studio context changed to ${gitProvenance.branch} (${gitProvenance.repo}). Switch context in Home, then retry.`
   306	    );
   307	  }
   308	
   309	  await updateStudioPolicyMode(policyMode, { repoRoot });
   310	  const resolved = await resolveReviewContext({
   311	    commitish: 'HEAD',
   312	    projectRoot: '.',
   313	    lastCheckpoints,
   314	    signal: options.signal,
   315	    onProgress: (event) => emitEvent({
   316	      type: 'stage',
   317	      stage: event.stage,
   318	      state: event.state,
   319	      label: event.label,
   320	      detail: event.detail,
   321	    }),
   322	  });
   323	
   324	  await emitEvent({
   325	    type: 'stage',
   326	    stage: 'review_creation',
   327	    state: 'active',
   328	    label: policyMode === 'review' ? 'Creating policy review' : 'Creating review',
   329	    detail:
   330	      policyMode === 'review'
   331	        ? 'Creating the review and opening the policy screen.'
   332	        : 'Creating the review record before queueing analysis.',
   333	  });
   334	
   335	  if (policyMode === 'review') {
   336	    throwIfAborted();
   337	    const derived = await deriveReviewPolicy(workerUrl, {
   338	      workspaceId: resolved.workspaceId,
   339	      deploymentId: resolved.deploymentId,
   340	      policyMode: 'review',
   341	      reviewBasis: 'checkpoint',
   342	      provenance: resolved.resolvedProvenance,
   343	    });
   344	    // Once the review exists, finish the handoff even if the client disconnects.
   345	    abortBeforeReviewCreation = false;
   346	
   347	    await emitEvent({
   348	      type: 'stage',
   349	      stage: 'review_creation',
   350	      state: 'completed',
   351	      label: 'Policy review ready',
   352	      detail: `Review ${derived.reviewId} is ready for policy confirmation.`,
   353	    });
   354	
   355	    const result: StudioNewReviewStartResult = {
   356	      reviewId: derived.reviewId,
   357	      routePath: buildStudioReviewRoutePath({
   358	        reviewId: derived.reviewId,
   359	        route: 'policy',
   360	        repo: resolved.resolvedProvenance.repo,
   361	        branch: resolved.resolvedProvenance.branch,
   362	      }),
   363	      policyMode,
   364	      status: 'policy_ready',
   365	    };
   366	    await emitEvent({
   367	      type: 'completed',
   368	      ...result,
   369	      detail: 'Policy review is ready. Opening the policy screen.',
   370	    });
   371	    return result;
   372	  }
   373	
   374	  throwIfAborted();
   375	  const derived = await deriveReviewPolicy(workerUrl, {
   376	    workspaceId: resolved.workspaceId,
   377	    deploymentId: resolved.deploymentId,
   378	    policyMode: 'auto',
   379	    reviewBasis: 'checkpoint',
   380	    provenance: resolved.resolvedProvenance,
   381	  });
   382	  // Once the review exists, finish the handoff even if the client disconnects.
   383	  abortBeforeReviewCreation = false;
   384	  await emitEvent({
   385	    type: 'stage',
   386	    stage: 'review_creation',
   387	    state: 'completed',
   388	    label: 'Review created',
   389	    detail: `Review ${derived.reviewId} was created and is ready to queue.`,
   390	  });
   391	  await emitEvent({
   392	    type: 'stage',
   393	    stage: 'policy',
   394	    state: 'active',
   395	    label: 'Approving policy',
   396	    detail: 'Applying the derived policy so Nimbus can start analysis.',
   397	  });
   398	  await approveReviewPolicy(workerUrl, derived.reviewId, {
   399	    approvedPolicy: derived.derivedPolicy,
   400	  });
   401	  await emitEvent({
   402	    type: 'stage',
   403	    stage: 'policy',
   404	    state: 'completed',
   405	    label: 'Review queued',
   406	    detail: `Review ${derived.reviewId} is queued and ready to stream live activity.`,
   407	  });
   408	
   409	  const result: StudioNewReviewStartResult = {
   410	    reviewId: derived.reviewId,
   411	    routePath: buildStudioReviewRoutePath({
   412	      reviewId: derived.reviewId,
   413	      route: 'reports',
   414	      repo: resolved.resolvedProvenance.repo,
   415	      branch: resolved.resolvedProvenance.branch,
   416	    }),
   417	    policyMode,
   418	    status: 'queued',
   419	  };
   420	  await emitEvent({
   421	    type: 'completed',
   422	    ...result,
   423	    detail: 'Review queued. Opening the live results route.',
   424	  });
   425	  return result;
   426	}
   427	
   428	export async function emitStudioStartEventForTests(input: {
   429	  signal?: AbortSignal;
   430	  onEvent?: (event: StudioNewReviewStartStreamEvent) => void | Promise<void>;
   431	  event: StudioNewReviewStartStreamEvent;

   433	  if (input.signal?.aborted) {
   434	    const error = new Error('Studio review start aborted before completion.');
   435	    error.name = 'AbortError';

   437	  }
   438	  try {
   439	    await input.onEvent?.(input.event);
   440	  } catch {
   441	    // Best-effort only in tests as well.
   442	  }
   443	}

function recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(
  state: ReviewEvidenceState,
  action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }>,
  toolOutput: unknown,
  changedPaths: Set<string>
): void {
  if (action.tool === 'diff_summary') {
    state.diffSummaryUsed = true;
    return;
  }

  if (action.tool === 'read_file') {
    const readPath = normalizePath(action.args.path);
    if (readPath && changedPaths.has(readPath)) {
      state.readChangedPaths.add(readPath);
    }
    return;
  }

  if (action.tool === 'search_code') {
    state.searchUsed = true;
    const matches = Array.isArray(asRecord(toolOutput).matches) ? (asRecord(toolOutput).matches as unknown[]) : [];
    state.searchMatchedChangedPath = matches.some((match) => {
      const matchPath = normalizePath(asRecord(match).path);
      return Boolean(matchPath && changedPaths.has(matchPath));
    });
  }
}

function collectMissingEvidenceRequirements(input: {
  evidence: ReviewEvidenceState;
  changedPaths: string[];
}): string[] {
  const missing: string[] = [];
  const sensitiveChangedPaths = input.changedPaths.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));

  if (input.changedPaths.length > 0 && !input.evidence.diffSummaryUsed) {
    missing.push('Run diff_summary at least once before completing analysis.');
  }

  const requiredReadCoverage =
    input.changedPaths.length <= MAX_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE_REQUIREMENT
      ? input.changedPaths.length
      : Math.min(MIN_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE, input.changedPaths.length);
  if (input.evidence.readChangedPaths.size < requiredReadCoverage) {
    missing.push(
      `Read at least ${requiredReadCoverage} changed file(s) directly with read_file (currently ${input.evidence.readChangedPaths.size}).`
    );
  }

  const missingSensitivePaths = sensitiveChangedPaths.filter((path) => !input.evidence.readChangedPaths.has(path));
  if (missingSensitivePaths.length > 0) {
    missing.push(`Read sensitive changed file(s) directly: ${missingSensitivePaths.join(', ')}.`);
  }

  if (input.changedPaths.length > 0 && !input.evidence.searchUsed) {
    missing.push('Run search_code at least once before completing analysis.');
  }

  return missing;
}

function selectDeterministicReadPaths(changedPaths: string[]): string[] {
  const deduped = Array.from(new Set(changedPaths));
  const sensitive = deduped.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));
  const requiredCount =
    deduped.length <= MAX_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE_REQUIREMENT
      ? deduped.length
      : Math.min(MIN_DIRECT_CHANGED_FILE_COVERAGE, deduped.length);
  const selected = [...sensitive];
  for (const path of deduped) {
    if (selected.length >= Math.max(requiredCount, sensitive.length)) {
      break;
    }
    if (!selected.includes(path)) {
      selected.push(path);
    }
  }
  return selected.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_READ_PATHS);
}

async function runDeterministicEvidenceCollection(input: {
  sandbox: SandboxClient;
  policy: ReviewCommandPolicy;
  maxFileBytes: number;
  authoritativeDiffSnapshot: unknown;
  changedPaths: string[];
  changedPathsSet: Set<string>;

  maxDeterministicSteps: number;

}): Promise<number> {

    { type: 'tool', tool: 'diff_summary', args: { maxBytes: 64_000 } },

      type: 'tool' as const,
      tool: 'read_file' as const,
      args: { path, maxBytes: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 48_000) },
    })),
    { type: 'tool', tool: 'search_code', args: { query: 'function', path: '.', maxResults: 40 } },
  ];

  let deterministicStep = 0;
  for (const action of deterministicTools) {
    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
      break;
    }

+    assert.equal(result.type, 'complete');
+    assert.equal(capturedBody?.model, 'gpt-5.4');
+    assert.deepEqual(capturedBody?.reasoning, { effort: 'medium' });
+  } finally {
+    globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
+  }
+}

+import { strict as assert } from 'assert';
+import {
+  computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests,
+  extractDeterministicSearchQueriesForTests,
+  extractIntegrationSearchQueriesForTests,
+} from '../../src/lib/review-analysis.js';
+
+export function runReviewAnalysisTests(): void {
+  assert.deepEqual(computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(8), {
+    deterministicMaxSteps: 2,
+    providerMaxSteps: 6,
+  });
+  assert.deepEqual(computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(18), {
+    deterministicMaxSteps: 12,
+    providerMaxSteps: 6,
+  });
+
+  const queries = extractDeterministicSearchQueriesForTests([
+    {
+      path: 'packages/worker/src/api/reviews/recovery.ts',
+      content: `
+import { updateReviewRunStatus, replaceReviewFindings } from '../../lib/db.js';
+import { createReviewQueueMessage } from '../../lib/review-queue.js';
+
+export async function manuallyRecoverReviewRun() {
+  await updateReviewRunStatus();
+  await replaceReviewFindings();
+  return createReviewQueueMessage();
+}
+      `,
+      byteSize: 240,
+      source: 'changed',
+    },
+  ]);
+
+  assert.deepEqual(
+    queries,
+    ['updateReviewRunStatus', 'replaceReviewFindings', 'createReviewQueueMessage']
+  );
+
+  const integrationQueries = extractIntegrationSearchQueriesForTests([
+    {
+      path: 'packages/report-ui/src/components/ReportPage.tsx',
+      content: `
+const response = await fetch(\`${'${API_BASE}'}/api/reviews/\${encodeURIComponent(reviewId)}/recover\`, {
+  method: 'POST',
+});
+if (review.status === 'running') {
+  return null;
+}
+      `,
+      byteSize: 240,
+      source: 'changed',
+    },
+    {
+      path: 'packages/cli/src/app/reviews/ui-proxy.ts',
+      content: `
+import { startStudioNewReview } from './studio-create.js';
+
+await startStudioNewReview({
+  onEvent: async (event) => {
+    return event;
+  },
+});
+      `,
+      byteSize: 240,
+      source: 'changed',
+    },
+    {
+      path: 'packages/cli/src/app/reviews/context.ts',
+      content: `
+export async function emitResolveReviewProgress(options, event) {
+  await options?.onProgress?.(event);
+}
+      `,
+      byteSize: 120,
+      source: 'changed',
+    },
+  ]);
+
+  assert.deepEqual(
+    integrationQueries,
+    ['/recover', 'startStudioNewReview', 'onEvent']
+  );
+}

+import { strict as assert } from 'assert';
+import { finalizeSuccessfulReview } from '../../src/lib/review-runner/finalization.js';
+
+export async function runReviewFinalizationTests(): Promise<void> {
+  let nonStatusQueryTouched = false;
+
+  const env = {
+    DB: {
+      prepare(sql: string) {
+        if (/SELECT \* FROM review_runs WHERE id = \?/i.test(sql)) {
+          return {
+            bind() {
+              return {
+                async first() {
+                  return {
+                    id: 'rev_abcd1234',
+                    workspace_id: 'ws_123',
+                    deployment_id: 'dep_123',
+                    target_type: 'workspace_deployment',
+                    mode: 'report_only',
+                    status: 'failed',
+                    idempotency_key: 'idem_123',
+                    request_payload_json: JSON.stringify({
+                      provenance: {
+                        repo: 'dayhaysoos/nimbus',
+                        branch: 'main',
+                      },
+                    }),
+                    request_payload_sha256: 'a'.repeat(64),
+                    account_id: null,
+                    provenance_json: '{}',
+                    derived_policy_json: null,
+                    approved_policy_json: null,
+                    approved_policy_sha256: null,
+                    last_event_seq: 0,
+                    attempt_count: 1,
+                    started_at: '2026-04-09T00:00:00.000Z',
+                    finished_at: '2026-04-09T00:01:00.000Z',
+                    report_json: null,
+                    markdown_summary: null,
+                    error_code: 'review_execution_aborted',
+                    error_message: 'manual fail',
+                    created_at: '2026-04-09T00:00:00.000Z',
+                    updated_at: '2026-04-09T00:01:00.000Z',
+                  };
+                },
+              };
+            },
+          };
+        }
+
+        nonStatusQueryTouched = true;
+        return {
+          bind() {
+            return {
+              async first() {
+                return null;
+              },
+              async run() {
+                return { success: true, meta: { changes: 0 } };
+              },
+            };
+          },
+        };
+      },
+    },
+  };
+
+  await finalizeSuccessfulReview(
+    env as never,
+    'rev_abcd1234',
+    { provenance: { repo: 'dayhaysoos/nimbus', branch: 'main' } },
+    {
+      summary: {
+        recommendation: 'approve',
+        riskLevel: 'low',
+        findingCounts: { info: 0, low: 0, medium: 0, high: 0, critical: 0 },
+      },
+      findings: [],
+      evidence: [],
+      provenance: {
+        repo: 'dayhaysoos/nimbus',
+        branch: 'main',
+        sessionIds: [],
+        policyItems: [],
+        promptSummary: '',
+        transcriptUrl: null,
+      },
+      markdownSummary: '',
+      summaryText: '',
+      furtherPassesLowYield: true,
+    } as never
+  );
+
+  assert.equal(nonStatusQueryTouched, false);
+}

   161	      checkpointId: null,
   162	      commitSha: null,
   163	      includedCheckpoints: [],
   164	      ready: false,
   165	      checks: [
   166	        {
   167	          code: 'checkpoint',
   168	          label: 'Checkpoint target',
   169	          ok: false,
   170	          detail: message,
   171	        },
   172	        {
   173	          code: 'entire_context',
   174	          label: 'Entire context',
   175	          ok: false,
   176	          detail: 'Blocked until checkpoint target is available.',
   177	        },
   178	      ],
   179	      error: {
   180	        code: preflightErrorCode(message),
   181	        message,
   182	      },
   183	    };
   184	  }
   185	
   186	  try {
   187	    const context = await validateReviewEntireIntentContext(
   188	      {
   189	        commitSha,
   190	        checkpointId,
   191	      },
   192	      {
   193	        summarizeSession: 'auto',

   195	      },

   215	          label: 'Checkpoint target',

   217	          detail: checkpointDetail,

   219	        {

   221	          label: 'Entire context',

   223	          detail: contextDetail,

   227	  } catch (error) {

   230	      repo,
   231	      branch,
   232	      policyMode,
   233	      lastCheckpoints,

   253	      error: {
   254	        code: preflightErrorCode(message),
   255	        message,
   256	      },
   257	    };
   258	  }
   259	}

    51	function mergeCheckpointContexts(input: {
    52	  contexts: ReviewEntireContextResolution[];
    53	  includedCheckpoints: IncludedCheckpointSummary[];
    54	}): ReviewEntireContextResolution {
    55	  const earliest = input.contexts[0];
    56	  const latest = input.contexts[input.contexts.length - 1];
    57	  const sessionIds = Array.from(new Set(input.contexts.flatMap((entry) => entry.context.sessionIds)));
    58	  const intentSessionContext = Array.from(
    59	    new Set(input.contexts.flatMap((entry) => entry.context.intentSessionContext.map((line) => line.trim()).filter(Boolean)))
    60	  );
    61	  const rawSessionPrompts = input.contexts
    62	    .map((entry) => entry.context.rawSessionPrompts)
    63	    .filter((value): value is string => typeof value === 'string' && value.trim().length > 0)
    64	    .join('\n\n---\n\n');
    65	
    66	  return {
    67	    context: {
    68	      note: `Review context merged from ${input.includedCheckpoints.length} checkpoints (${input.includedCheckpoints
    69	        .map((entry) => entry.checkpointId)
    70	        .join(' -> ')}).`,
    71	      sessionIds,
    72	      transcriptUrl: latest.context.transcriptUrl ?? earliest.context.transcriptUrl,
    73	      intentSessionContext,
    74	      rawSessionPrompts: rawSessionPrompts || null,
    75	    },
    76	    contextResolution: latest.contextResolution,
    77	    originalCheckpointId: earliest.originalCheckpointId,
    78	    resolvedCheckpointId: latest.resolvedCheckpointId,
    79	    resolvedCommitSha: latest.resolvedCommitSha,
    80	    resolvedCommitSubject: latest.resolvedCommitSubject,
    81	    commitsAgo: latest.commitsAgo,
    82	    fallbackReason: latest.fallbackReason,
    83	  };
    84	}

   114	async function emitResolveReviewProgress(
   115	  options: ResolveReviewContextOptions | undefined,
   116	  event: ResolveReviewContextProgressEvent
   117	): Promise<void> {
   118	  try {
   119	    await options?.onProgress?.(event);
   120	  } catch {
   121	    // Progress listeners are best-effort only and must not abort review setup.
   122	  }
   123	}
   124	
   125	function throwIfResolveReviewAborted(options: ResolveReviewContextOptions | undefined): void {
   126	  if (!options?.signal?.aborted) {
   127	    return;
   128	  }
   129	  const error = new Error('Review flow aborted before completion.');
   130	  error.name = 'AbortError';
   131	  throw error;
   132	}

   176	/**
   177	 * Orchestrates review context setup from a commit by validating Entire metadata,

   180	export async function resolveReviewContext(
   181	  options?: ResolveReviewContextOptions
   182	): Promise<ResolveReviewContextResult> {
   183	  throwIfResolveReviewAborted(options);
   184	  const commitish = options?.commitish?.trim() || 'HEAD';

   186	  const spinner: SpinnerLike =
   187	    process.stdout.isTTY && process.stderr.isTTY
   188	      ? p.spinner()

   190	          start: () => undefined,
   191	          message: () => undefined,
   192	          stop: () => undefined,
   193	        };

   196	  let checkpointId = '';

   212	        sessionsScanned: number;

   214	      }

   219	  spinner.start('Resolving checkpoint...');

   221	    throwIfResolveReviewAborted(options);

   223	      stage: 'checkpoint',
   224	      state: 'active',
   225	      label: 'Resolving checkpoint',

   227	    });
   228	    gitProvenance = resolveReviewGitProvenance();

   230	      baseRef: options?.baseRef,
   231	      lastCheckpoints: options?.lastCheckpoints,

   233	    });
   234	    commitSha = resolvedCommit.commitSha;
   235	    checkpointId = resolvedCommit.checkpointId;
   236	    includedCheckpoints = resolvedCommit.includedCheckpoints ?? [];
   237	    checkpointSelectionMode = resolvedCommit.checkpointSelectionMode ?? 'latest';
   238	    changedPaths = parseChangedPathsFromDiff(resolvedCommit.commitDiffPatch);
   239	    const normalizedPatch = normalizeCommitDiffPatch(resolvedCommit.commitDiffPatch);
   240	    commitDiffPatch = normalizedPatch.patch;
   241	    commitDiffPatchSha256 = normalizedPatch.sha256;
   242	    commitDiffPatchTruncated = normalizedPatch.truncated;
   243	    commitDiffPatchOriginalChars = normalizedPatch.originalChars;
   244	    spinner.stop(`Resolved checkpoint ${checkpointId} from ${commitSha.slice(0, 12)}`);

   246	      stage: 'checkpoint',
   247	      state: 'completed',
   248	      label: 'Checkpoint resolved',
   249	      detail: `Resolved checkpoint ${checkpointId} from ${commitSha.slice(0, 12)}.`,
   250	    });

   252	    spinner.stop('Checkpoint resolution failed');
   253	    throw buildReviewFlowStageError('checkpoint resolution', error);
   254	  }

   257	  try {

   259	    await emitResolveReviewProgress(options, {
   260	      stage: 'entire_context',

    94	
    95	function statusFromReviewEventType(eventType: string): ReviewResponse['status'] | null {
    96	  switch (eventType) {
    97	    case 'review_created':
    98	    case 'review_enqueued':
    99	      return 'queued';
   100	    case 'review_succeeded':
   101	      return 'succeeded';
   102	    case 'review_failed':
   103	      return 'failed';
   104	    case 'review_cancelled':
   105	      return 'cancelled';
   106	    case 'review_policy_approved':
   107	      return 'policy_approved';
   108	    default:
   109	      return null;
   110	  }
   111	}
   112	
   113	function eventToLogEntry(eventType: string, data: Record<string, unknown>): ActivityLogEntry | null {
   114	  if (eventType === 'heartbeat' || eventType === 'snapshot' || eventType === 'terminal') {
   115	    return null;
   116	  }
   117	  const label = EVENT_LABELS[eventType] ?? eventType.replace(/_/g, ' ');
   118	  let detail: string | undefined;
   119	
   120	  if (eventType === 'review_analysis_provider_request_started' && typeof data.step === 'number') {
   121	    detail = `Step ${data.step}`;
   122	  } else if (eventType === 'review_analysis_model_output_received' && typeof data.step === 'number') {
   123	    detail = data.repairAttempted ? `Step ${data.step} (repair)` : `Step ${data.step}`;
   124	  } else if (eventType === 'review_finding_emitted') {
   125	    detail = typeof data.description === 'string' ? data.description : undefined;
   126	  } else if (eventType === 'review_context_changed_files_collected' && typeof data.changedFileCount === 'number') {
   127	    detail = `${data.changedFileCount} files`;
   128	  } else if (eventType === 'review_analysis_agent_started' && typeof data.model === 'string') {
   129	    detail = data.model;
   130	  } else if (eventType === 'review_context_budget_checked') {
   131	    if (typeof data.estimatedTokens === 'number' && typeof data.tokenBudget === 'number') {
   132	      detail = `${data.estimatedTokens.toLocaleString()} / ${data.tokenBudget.toLocaleString()} tokens`;
   133	    }
   134	  } else if (eventType === 'review_failed' && typeof data.message === 'string') {
   135	    detail = data.message;
   136	  } else if (eventType === 'review_succeeded' && typeof data.findingCount === 'number') {
   137	    detail = `${data.findingCount} finding${data.findingCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
   138	  }
   139	
   140	  return {
   141	    id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
   142	    timestamp: Date.now(),
   143	    label,
   144	    detail,
   145	  };
   146	}

   149	  key: string;

   154	}

   161	
   162	function severityColor(severity: ReviewFinding['severity']): string {
   163	  switch (severity) {
   164	    case 'critical':
   165	      return 'text-red-700 bg-red-50 border-red-200';
   166	    case 'high':
   167	      return 'text-orange-700 bg-orange-50 border-orange-200';
   168	    case 'medium':
   169	      return 'text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 border-amber-200';
   170	    case 'low':
   171	      return 'text-sky-700 bg-sky-50 border-sky-200';
   172	    default:
   173	      return 'text-slate-600 bg-slate-50 border-slate-200';
   174	  }
   175	}
   176	
   177	function severityBarColor(severity: Exclude<ReviewSeverity, 'info'>): string {
   178	  if (severity === 'critical') {
   179	    return 'bg-red-500';
   180	  }
   181	  if (severity === 'high') {
   182	    return 'bg-orange-500';
   183	  }
   184	  if (severity === 'medium') {
   185	    return 'bg-amber-500';
   186	  }
   187	  return 'bg-sky-500';
   188	}
   189	
   190	function recommendationTone(recommendation: string | undefined): {
   191	  containerClass: string;
   192	  labelClass: string;
   193	} {
   194	  if (recommendation === 'approve') {
   195	    return {
   196	      containerClass: 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/45',
   197	      labelClass: 'text-emerald-900',
   198	    };
   199	  }
   200	  if (recommendation === 'comment') {
   201	    return {
   202	      containerClass: 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/50',
   203	      labelClass: 'text-amber-900',
   204	    };
   205	  }
   206	  if (recommendation === 'request_changes') {
   207	    return {
   208	      containerClass: 'border-red-200 bg-red-50/50',
   209	      labelClass: 'text-red-900',
   210	    };
   211	  }
   212	  return {
   213	    containerClass: 'border-border bg-card/70',
   214	    labelClass: 'text-foreground',
   215	  };
   216	}
   217	
   218	function phaseStateClass(state: TimelinePhaseState): string {
   219	  if (state === 'completed') {
   220	    return 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800';
   221	  }
   222	  if (state === 'active') {
   223	    return 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-800';
   224	  }

   229	  if (riskLevel === 'critical') {
   230	    return 'border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-800';
   231	  }
   232	  if (riskLevel === 'high') {
   233	    return 'border-orange-200 bg-orange-50 text-orange-800';

   235	  if (riskLevel === 'medium') {
   236	    return 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-800';
   237	  }
   238	  if (riskLevel === 'low') {
   239	    return 'border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 text-sky-800';
   240	  }
   241	  return 'border-border bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground';
   242	}
   243

   245	  if (status === 'passed') {
   246	    return 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800';
   247	  }
   248	  if (status === 'failed') {
   249	    return 'border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-800';
   250	  }
   251	  if (status === 'warning') {

     3	  appendReviewEvent,
