    10	import { jsonResponse } from './shared.js';
    11	
    12	const REVIEW_STALE_RUNNING_GRACE_MS = 60_000;
    13	export const REVIEW_STALE_NOAUTH_TERMINAL_GRACE_MS = 120_000;
    14	
    15	function parseTimeoutMs(value: string | undefined, fallback: number): number {
    16	  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
    17	    return fallback;
    18	  }
    19	  const parsed = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
    20	  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed <= 0) {
    21	    return fallback;
    22	  }
    23	  return parsed;
    24	}
    25	
    26	function parseMaxRetryCount(value: string | undefined, fallbackAttempts: number): number {
    27	  const parsedAttempts = Number.parseInt(value ?? '', 10);
    28	  const attempts = Number.isFinite(parsedAttempts) && parsedAttempts > 0 ? parsedAttempts : fallbackAttempts;
    29	  return Math.max(0, attempts - 1);
    30	}
    31	
    32	function hasLocalCochangeProvenance(payload: unknown): boolean {
    33	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object' || Array.isArray(payload)) {
    34	    return false;
    35	  }
    36	  const record = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
    37	  const provenance = record.provenance;
    38	  if (!provenance || typeof provenance !== 'object' || Array.isArray(provenance)) {
    39	    return false;
    40	  }
    41	  const localCochange = (provenance as Record<string, unknown>).localCochange;
    42	  if (!localCochange || typeof localCochange !== 'object' || Array.isArray(localCochange)) {
    43	    return false;
    44	  }
    45	  const candidate = localCochange as Record<string, unknown>;
    46	  if (candidate.source !== 'local_git') {
    47	    return false;
    48	  }
    49	  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(candidate, 'relatedByChangedPath')) {
    50	    return false;
    51	  }
    52	  const relatedByChangedPath = candidate.relatedByChangedPath;
    53	  return Boolean(relatedByChangedPath !== null && typeof relatedByChangedPath === 'object' && !Array.isArray(relatedByChangedPath));
    54	}
    55	
    56	function isValidScopedGithubToken(value: string): boolean {
    57	  const token = value.trim();
    58	  if (!token) {
    59	    return false;
    60	  }
    61	  return /^(ghp_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}|github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,})$/.test(token);
    62	}

    64	export async function recoverStaleRunningReviewIfNeeded(
    65	  env: Env,
    66	  reviewId: string,
    67	  review: { status: ReviewRunStatus; startedAt: string | null; updatedAt: string; createdAt: string; attemptCount: number },
    68	  cochangeGithubToken?: string | null,
    69	  openrouterApiKey?: string | null,
    70	  options?: { markFailedWhenRetryUnavailable?: boolean; noAuthTerminalGraceMs?: number }
    71	): Promise<void> {
    72	  if (review.status !== 'running') {
    73	    return;
    74	  }
    75	  const attemptTimeoutMs = parseTimeoutMs(env.ATTEMPT_TIMEOUT_MS, 600_000);
    76	  const staleThresholdMs = attemptTimeoutMs + REVIEW_STALE_RUNNING_GRACE_MS;
    77	  const startedMs = Date.parse(review.startedAt ?? review.updatedAt ?? review.createdAt);
    78	  if (!Number.isFinite(startedMs)) {
    79	    return;
    80	  }
    81	  const staleForMs = Date.now() - startedMs;
    82	  if (staleForMs < staleThresholdMs) {
    83	    return;
    84	  }
    85	
    86	  const maxRetries = parseMaxRetryCount(env.MAX_ATTEMPTS, 3);
    87	  const requestPayload = await getReviewRunRequestPayload(env.DB, reviewId);
    88	  const canRetryWithoutGithubToken=[REDACTED](requestPayload);
    89	  const rawScopedToken = typeof cochangeGithubToken === 'string' && cochangeGithubToken.trim() ? cochangeGithubToken.trim() : null;
    90	  const scopedGithubToken = rawScopedToken && isValidScopedGithubToken(rawScopedToken) ? rawScopedToken : null;
    91	  const hasInvalidScopedToken = Boolean(rawScopedToken) && !scopedGithubToken;
    92	  if (review.attemptCount <= maxRetries && env.REVIEWS_QUEUE && (scopedGithubToken || canRetryWithoutGithubToken)) {
    93	    await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'queued', {
    94	      report: null,
    95	      markdownSummary: null,
    96	      startedAt: null,
    97	      finishedAt: null,
    98	      errorCode: 'retry_scheduled',
    99	      errorMessage: `Review execution stalled in running state for ${Math.floor(staleForMs / 1000)}s.`,
   100	    });
   101	    await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
   102	      reviewId,
   103	      eventType: 'review_retry_scheduled',
   104	      payload: {
   105	        attemptCount: review.attemptCount,
   106	        maxRetries,
   107	        reason: 'stale_running_timeout',
   108	        staleForSeconds: Math.floor(staleForMs / 1000),
   109	        authMode: scopedGithubToken ? 'scoped_request_token=[REDACTED]',
   110	      },
   111	    });
   112	    await env.REVIEWS_QUEUE.send(createReviewQueueMessage(reviewId, scopedGithubToken, openrouterApiKey));
   113	    return;
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  if (options?.markFailedWhenRetryUnavailable === false) {
   117	    const noAuthTerminalGraceMs =
   118	      typeof options.noAuthTerminalGraceMs === 'number' && options.noAuthTerminalGraceMs >= 0
   119	        ? options.noAuthTerminalGraceMs
   120	        : REVIEW_STALE_NOAUTH_TERMINAL_GRACE_MS;
   121	    if (staleForMs < staleThresholdMs + noAuthTerminalGraceMs) {
   122	      return;
   123	    }
   124	  }
   125	
   126	  const missingTokenSuffix =
   127	    !scopedGithubToken && !canRetryWithoutGithubToken
   128	      ? ' No retry was scheduled because a fresh scoped GitHub token was not provided. Re-run review creation with X-Review-Github-Token (CLI: set REVIEW_CONTEXT_GITHUB_TOKEN).'
   129	      : '';
   130	  const invalidTokenSuffix = hasInvalidScopedToken
   131	    ? ' No retry was scheduled because the provided scoped GitHub token format is invalid.'
   132	    : '';
   133	  const retriesExhaustedSuffix = review.attemptCount > maxRetries ? ' No retry was scheduled because max retry attempts were exhausted.' : '';
   134	  const message = `Review execution timed out after ${Math.floor(staleForMs / 1000)}s in running state.${missingTokenSuffix}${invalidTokenSuffix}${retriesExhaustedSuffix}`;
   135	  await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'failed', {
   136	    report: null,
   137	    markdownSummary: null,
   138	    errorCode: 'review_execution_timeout',
   139	    errorMessage: message,
   140	  });
   141	  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
   142	    reviewId,
   143	    eventType: 'review_failed',
   144	    payload: {
   145	      code: 'review_execution_timeout',
   146	      message,
   147	    },
   148	  });
   149	}

   151	export async function validateRecoveredReviewRetryAuth(
   152	  env: Env,
   153	  reviewId: string,
   154	  shouldReenqueueRecoveredReview: boolean,
   155	  reviewGithubToken: string | null
   156	): Promise<Response | null> {
   157	  if (!shouldReenqueueRecoveredReview) {
   158	    return null;
   159	  }
   160	
   161	  if (reviewGithubToken && !isValidScopedGithubToken(reviewGithubToken)) {
   162	    return jsonResponse(
   163	      {
   164	        error: 'Scoped GitHub token format is invalid for retry. Expected ghp_* or github_pat_* token.',
   165	        code: 'invalid_token_format',
   166	      },
   167	      409
   168	    );
   169	  }
   170	
   171	  if (reviewGithubToken) {
   172	    return null;
   173	  }
   174	
   175	  const storedRequestPayload = await getReviewRunRequestPayload(env.DB, reviewId);
   176	  if (hasLocalCochangeProvenance(storedRequestPayload)) {
   177	    return null;
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  return jsonResponse(
   181	    {
   182	      error:
   183	        'Scoped GitHub token required for retry. Provide X-Review-Github-Token (CLI: set REVIEW_CONTEXT_GITHUB_TOKEN) when re-queueing recovered reviews without local co-change provenance.',
   184	      code: 'review_context_github_token_missing',
   185	    },
   186	    409
   187	  );
   188	}

   125	
   126	function inferFollowUpReviewScore(output: { findings: ReviewFinding[]; furtherPassesLowYield: boolean }): 1 | 2 | 3 {
   127	  const severities = new Set(output.findings.map((finding) => finding.severity));
   128	  if (severities.has('critical') || severities.has('high')) {
   129	    return 3;
   130	  }
   131	  if (severities.has('medium')) {
   132	    return 2;
   133	  }
   134	  if (output.findings.length === 0) {
   135	    return output.furtherPassesLowYield ? 1 : 2;
   136	  }
   137	  return 1;
   138	}
   139	
   140	function deriveFollowUpReviewMetadata(
   141	  payload: unknown,
   142	  output: { findings: ReviewFinding[]; furtherPassesLowYield: boolean }
   143	): { score: 1 | 2 | 3; rationale: string } {
   144	  const record = asRecord(payload);
   145	  const rawScore = typeof record.followUpReviewScore === 'number' ? Math.floor(record.followUpReviewScore) : null;
   146	  const score = rawScore === 1 || rawScore === 2 || rawScore === 3 ? rawScore : inferFollowUpReviewScore(output);
   147	  const rationaleCandidate = typeof record.followUpReviewRationale === 'string' ? record.followUpReviewRationale.trim() : '';
   148	  const rationale =
   149	    rationaleCandidate ||
   150	    (score === 3
   151	      ? 'High-severity findings remain in changed paths; another review pass is required after fixes.'
   152	      : score === 2
   153	        ? 'At least one non-trivial issue remains; a follow-up review pass is recommended after fixes.'
   154	        : 'Findings are low severity or low signal; additional review passes are likely diminishing returns.');
   155	
   156	  return { score, rationale };
   157	}

   170	function parseCompleteActionPayload(action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'complete' }>): unknown {
   171	  if (action.finalOutput !== undefined && action.finalOutput !== null) {
   172	    return action.finalOutput;
   173	  }
   174	  if (typeof action.summary === 'string' && action.summary.trim()) {
   175	    return parseJsonOutput(action.summary);
   176	  }
   177	  throw new ReviewAgentOutputError(
   178	    'Review agent complete action requires finalOutput or a summary containing valid JSON payload'
   179	  ).withCode(
   180	    'review_analysis_invalid_output',
   181	    {
   182	      errors: [
   183	        {
   184	          path: '$',
   185	          message: 'Complete action must include finalOutput or summary containing valid JSON payload.',
   186	        },
   187	      ],
   188	    }
   189	  );
   190	}
   191	
   192	function isSensitiveChangedPath(path: string): boolean {
   193	  return /(?:recovery|retry|queue|status|state|workflow|db|auth)/i.test(path);
   194	}

   197	  return {
   198	    diffSummaryUsed: false,

   205	
   206	function parseReviewReasoningEffort(value: string | null | undefined): ReviewReasoningEffort | undefined {
   207	  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
   208	    return undefined;
   209	  }
   210	  const normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   211	  return normalized === 'minimal' || normalized === 'low' || normalized === 'medium' || normalized === 'high'

   214	}

   216	function normalizePath(value: unknown): string | null {
   217	  if (typeof value !== 'string') {

   219	  }
   220	  const trimmed = value.trim().replace(/\\/g, '/');
   221	  return trimmed ? trimmed : null;
   222	}

   224	function recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(
   225	  state: ReviewEvidenceState,
   226	  action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }>,
   227	  toolOutput: unknown,
   228	  changedPaths: Set<string>
   229	): void {
   230	  if (action.tool === 'diff_summary') {
   231	    state.diffSummaryUsed = true;
   232	    return;

   234	
   235	  if (action.tool === 'read_file') {
   236	    const readPath = normalizePath(action.args.path);
   237	    if (readPath) {

   239	        state.readChangedPaths.add(readPath);

   241	        state.readCrossFilePaths.add(readPath);
   242	      }

   244	    return;
   245	  }
   246	
   247	  if (action.tool === 'read_batch') {
   248	    const files = Array.isArray(asRecord(toolOutput).files) ? (asRecord(toolOutput).files as unknown[]) : [];

   250	      const readPath = normalizePath(asRecord(file).path);
   251	      if (!readPath) {
   252	        continue;
   253	      }
   254	      if (changedPaths.has(readPath)) {
   255	        state.readChangedPaths.add(readPath);
   256	      } else {
   257	        state.readCrossFilePaths.add(readPath);
   258	      }
   259	    }
   260	    return;

    18	/**

    21	export async function finalizeSuccessfulReview(
    22	  env: Env,
    23	  reviewId: string,
    24	  payload: Record<string, unknown>,
    25	  report: ReviewReport
    26	): Promise<void> {
    27	  const latest = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
    28	  if (!latest || latest.status !== 'running') {
    29	    return;
    30	  }
    31	
    32	  const payloadRecord = asRecord(payload);
    33	  const requestProvenance = asRecord(payloadRecord.provenance);
    34	  const reviewRepo = readOptionalString(requestProvenance.repo);
    35	  const reviewBranch = readOptionalString(requestProvenance.branch);
    36	  if (!reviewRepo || !reviewBranch) {
    37	    throw new Error('Review request payload missing required provenance.repo or provenance.branch.');
    38	  }
    39	
    40	  const findingSequenceStart = (await getHighestFindingNumberForBranch(env.DB, reviewRepo, reviewBranch)) + 1;
    41	  const findingsWithSequence = report.findings.map((finding, index) => ({
    42	    ...finding,
    43	    sequence: findingSequenceStart + index,
    44	  }));
    45	  const reportWithSequence: ReviewReport = {
    46	    ...report,
    47	    findings: findingsWithSequence,
    48	  };

    51	    reviewId,
    52	    eventType: 'review_finalize_started',
    53	    payload: {
    54	      findingCount: report.findings.length,
    55	    },
    56	  });
    57	  await replaceReviewFindings(env.DB, reviewId, findingsWithSequence, { startNumber: findingSequenceStart });
    58	  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    59	    reviewId,
    60	    eventType: 'review_analysis_findings_persisted',
    61	    payload: {
    62	      findingCount: report.findings.length,
    63	    },
    64	  });
    65	  await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'succeeded', {
    66	    report: reportWithSequence,
    67	    markdownSummary: reportWithSequence.markdownSummary,
    68	    errorCode: null,
    69	    errorMessage: null,
    70	  });
    71	  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    72	    reviewId,
    73	    eventType: 'review_analysis_succeeded',
    74	    payload: {
    75	      findingCount: report.findings.length,
    76	    },

    78	  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {

    83	      findingCount: report.findings.length,

    36	/**
    37	 * Claims, executes, persists, and finalizes one review run.
    38	 * This is the main orchestration boundary for review execution and retry scheduling.
    39	 */
    40	export async function processReviewRun(env: Env, reviewId: string, options?: ReviewRunExecutionOptions): Promise<void> {
    41	  const claimed = await claimReviewRunForExecution(env.DB, reviewId);
    42	  if (!claimed) {
    43	    const existing = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
    44	    if (existing?.status === 'running') {
    45	      await handleUnclaimedReviewRun(env, reviewId, existing, options?.allowRetryScheduling ?? true);
    46	    }
    47	    return;
    48	  }
    49	
    50	  let review: ReviewRunResponse | null = null;
    51	  try {
    52	    review = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
    53	    if (!review) {
    54	      return;
    55	    }
    56	
    57	    const payload = await getReviewRunRequestPayload(env.DB, reviewId);
    58	    if (!payload) {
    59	      await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'failed', {
    60	        errorCode: 'review_not_found',
    61	        errorMessage: 'Review request payload no longer exists',
    62	      });
    63	      await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
    64	        reviewId,
    65	        eventType: 'review_failed',
    66	        payload: {
    67	          code: 'review_not_found',
    68	          message: 'Review request payload no longer exists',
    69	        },
    70	      });
    71	      return;
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    const reviewContext = await assembleReviewContextBootstrap(env, review, payload, options);
    75	    const report = await executeReviewRun(env, review, payload, reviewContext, options);
    76	    await finalizeSuccessfulReview(env, reviewId, payload, report);
    77	  } catch (error) {
    78	    const message = formatReviewAnalysisError(error, {
    79	      openrouterApiKey=[REDACTED](options?.openrouterApiKey),
    80	    });
    81	    const latest = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
    82	    const attemptCount = latest?.attemptCount ?? review?.attemptCount ?? 0;
    83	
    84	    const allowRetryScheduling = options?.allowRetryScheduling !== false;
    85	    if (allowRetryScheduling && shouldRetryReviewError(error) && attemptCount <= 2) {
    86	      await scheduleReviewRetry(env, reviewId, {
    87	        attemptCount,
    88	        message,
    89	        reason: message.slice(0, 500),
    90	        throwMessage: 'Review transient failure; retry requested',
    91	      });
    92	    }
    93	
    94	    const contextAssemblyErrorCode = error instanceof ReviewContextAssemblyError ? error.code : null;
    95	    const finalErrorCode = contextAssemblyErrorCode ?? 'review_execution_failed';
    96	    await finalizeFailedReview(env, reviewId, {
    97	      errorCode: finalErrorCode,
    98	      message,
    99	      contextAssemblyErrorCode,
   100	    });
   101	  }
   102	}
   103	
   104	/**
   105	 * Repeats inline review execution while the persisted state requests another retry cycle.
   106	 */
   107	export async function runReviewInlineWithRetries(
   108	  env: Env,
   109	  reviewId: string,
   110	  maxCycles = 4,
   111	  options?: ReviewRunExecutionOptions
   112	): Promise<void> {
   113	  for (let cycle = 0; cycle < maxCycles; cycle += 1) {
   114	    try {
   115	      await processReviewRun(env, reviewId, options);
   116	    } catch {
   117	      // Retry scheduling is inferred from persisted status.
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    const latest = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
   121	    if (!latest) {
   122	      return;
   123	    }
   124	    if (latest.status !== 'queued') {
   125	      return;
   126	    }
   127	    if (latest.error?.code !== 'retry_scheduled') {
   128	      return;
   129	    }
   130	  }
   131	
   132	  await finalizeInlineRetryExhaustion(env, reviewId);
   133	}

   311	function selectDeterministicReadPaths(changedPaths: string[]): string[] {
   312	  const deduped = Array.from(new Set(changedPaths));

   366	function maybeAddDeterministicSearchQuery(

   431	function extractStaticApiTailQueries(content: string): string[] {

   445	      }
   446	      queries.push(`/${segment}`);
   447	      break;
   448	    }
   449	  }
   450	  return queries;
   451	}

   516	  return queries.slice(0, MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES);

   532	  kind: DeterministicSearchKind;
   533	  query: string;
   534	}): number {
   535	  let score = pathDistanceScore(input.sourcePath, input.candidatePath);
   536	  if (input.kind === 'api_route') {
   537	    if (/\/src\/api\//.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   538	      score -= 6;
   539	    }
   540	    if (/packages\/worker\//.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   541	      score -= 3;
   542	    }
   543	    if (/recover|fail|retry|events/i.test(input.query) && /recover|fail|retry|events/i.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   544	      score -= 3;
   545	    }
   546	    if (/reviews/i.test(input.candidatePath)) {
   547	      score -= 1;
   548	    }
   549	    return score;
   550	  }
   551	
   552	  if (input.kind === 'imported_symbol' || input.kind === 'callback') {
   553	    if (topLevelPackage(input.candidatePath) === topLevelPackage(input.sourcePath)) {
   554	      score -= 4;
   555	    }
   556	    if (input.candidatePath.split('/').slice(0, 3).join('/') === input.sourcePath.split('/').slice(0, 3).join('/')) {
   557	      score -= 2;
   558	    }
   559	  }
   560	
   561	  return score;
   562	}
   563	
   564	function combineDeterministicSearchQueries(
   565	  primary: DeterministicSearchQuery[],
   566	  secondary: DeterministicSearchQuery[]
   567	): DeterministicSearchQuery[] {
   568	  const combined: DeterministicSearchQuery[] = [];
   569	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   570	  for (const query of [...primary, ...secondary]) {
   571	    const lowered = query.query.trim().toLowerCase();
   572	    if (!lowered || seen.has(lowered)) {
   573	      continue;
   574	    }
   575	    seen.add(lowered);
   576	    combined.push(query);
   577	    if (combined.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_SEARCH_QUERIES) {
   578	      break;
   579	    }
   580	  }
   581	  return combined;
   582	}
   583	
   584	function pathDistanceScore(sourcePath: string, candidatePath: string): number {
   585	  const sourceSegments = sourcePath.split('/');
   586	  const candidateSegments = candidatePath.split('/');
   587	  let shared = 0;
   588	  while (
   589	    shared < sourceSegments.length &&
   590	    shared < candidateSegments.length &&
   591	    sourceSegments[shared] === candidateSegments[shared]
   592	  ) {
   593	    shared += 1;
   594	  }
   595	  return (sourceSegments.length - shared) + (candidateSegments.length - shared);
   596	}
   597	
   598	export function computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(maxSteps: number): { deterministicMaxSteps: number; providerMaxSteps: number } {
   599	  const providerMaxSteps = Math.max(1, maxSteps - Math.max(0, maxSteps - MIN_PROVIDER_REASONING_STEPS));
   600	  const deterministicMaxSteps = Math.max(0, maxSteps - providerMaxSteps);
   601	  return { deterministicMaxSteps, providerMaxSteps };
   602	}
   603	
   604	async function runDeterministicEvidenceCollection(input: {
   605	  sandbox: SandboxClient;
   606	  policy: ReviewCommandPolicy;
   607	  maxFileBytes: number;
   608	  authoritativeDiffSnapshot: unknown;
   609	  changedPaths: string[];
   610	  changedFiles: ReviewContext['retrieval']['changedFiles'];
   611	  changedPathsSet: Set<string>;
   612	  evidence: ReviewEvidenceState;
   613	  usedTools: string[];
   614	  history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[];
   615	  maxDeterministicSteps: number;
   616	  onLifecycleEvent?: (eventType: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) => void | Promise<void>;
   617	}): Promise<number> {
   618	  const deterministicTools: Array<Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }>> = [
   619	    { type: 'tool', tool: 'diff_summary', args: { maxBytes: 64_000 } },
   620	    ...selectDeterministicReadPaths(input.changedPaths).map((path) => ({
   620	    ...selectDeterministicReadPaths(input.changedPaths).map((path) => ({
   621	      type: 'tool' as const,
   622	      tool: 'read_file' as const,
   623	      args: { path, maxBytes: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 48_000) },
   624	    })),
   625	  ];
   626	  const followUpReadPaths: string[] = [];
   627	
   628	  let deterministicStep = 0;
   629	  for (const action of deterministicTools) {
   630	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   631	      break;
   632	    }
   633	    deterministicStep += 1;
   634	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   635	      input.sandbox,
   636	      action,
   637	      input.policy,
   638	      input.maxFileBytes,
   639	      action.tool === 'diff_summary' ? input.authoritativeDiffSnapshot : undefined
   640	    );
   641	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   642	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   643	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   644	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   645	        step: deterministicStep,
   646	        tool: action.tool,
   647	        deterministic: true,
   648	      });
   649	    }
   650	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   651	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   652	  }
   653	
   654	  const deterministicSearchQueries = combineDeterministicSearchQueries(
   655	    extractIntegrationSearchQueries(input.changedFiles),
   656	    extractDeterministicSearchQueries(input.changedFiles)
   657	  );
   658	  for (const searchQuery of deterministicSearchQueries) {
   659	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   660	      break;
   661	    }
   662	    const action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }> = {
   663	      type: 'tool',
   664	      tool: 'search_code',
   665	      args: {
   666	        query: searchQuery.query,
   667	        path: '.',
   668	        maxResults: 20,
   669	        maxBytesPerFile: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 16_000),
   670	      },
   671	    };
   672	    deterministicStep += 1;
   673	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   674	      input.sandbox,
   675	      action,
   676	      input.policy,
   677	      input.maxFileBytes,
   678	    );
   679	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   680	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   681	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   682	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   683	        step: deterministicStep,
   684	        tool: action.tool,
   685	        deterministic: true,
   686	        query: searchQuery.query,
   687	      });
   688	    }
   689	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   690	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   691	
   692	    const matches = Array.isArray(asRecord(output.result).matches) ? (asRecord(output.result).matches as unknown[]) : [];
   693	    const rankedMatches = matches
   694	      .map((match) => {
   695	        const matchPath = normalizePath(asRecord(match).path);
   696	        return matchPath
   697	          ? {
   698	              matchPath,
   699	              score: scoreSearchMatch({
   700	                sourcePath: searchQuery.sourcePath,
   701	                candidatePath: matchPath,
   702	                kind: searchQuery.kind,
   703	                query: searchQuery.query,
   704	              }),
   705	            }
   706	          : null;
   707	      })
   708	      .filter((match): match is { matchPath: string; score: number } => Boolean(match))
   709	      .sort((left, right) => left.score - right.score);
   710	    for (const match of rankedMatches) {
   711	      const matchPath = match.matchPath;
   712	      if (input.changedPathsSet.has(matchPath) || followUpReadPaths.includes(matchPath)) {
   713	        continue;
   714	      }
   715	      followUpReadPaths.push(matchPath);
   716	      if (followUpReadPaths.length >= MAX_DETERMINISTIC_CROSS_FILE_READS) {
   717	        break;
   718	      }
   719	    }
   720	  }
   721	
   722	  for (const path of followUpReadPaths) {
   723	    if (deterministicStep >= input.maxDeterministicSteps) {
   724	      break;
   725	    }
   726	    const batchPaths = [path, ...followUpReadPaths.filter((candidate) => candidate !== path)].slice(0, 2);
   727	    const action: Extract<ReviewAgentAction, { type: 'tool' }> = {
   728	      type: 'tool',
   729	      tool: 'read_batch',
   730	      args: { paths: batchPaths, maxBytes: Math.min(input.maxFileBytes, 32_000) },
   731	    };
   732	    deterministicStep += 1;
   733	    const output = await executeReviewTool(
   734	      input.sandbox,
   735	      action,
   736	      input.policy,
   737	      input.maxFileBytes,
   738	    );
   739	    input.usedTools.push(action.tool);
   740	    recordEvidenceFromToolExecution(input.evidence, action, output.result, input.changedPathsSet);
   741	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   742	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_tool_executed', {
   743	        step: deterministicStep,
   744	        tool: action.tool,
   745	        deterministic: true,
   746	        crossFile: true,
   747	      });
   748	    }
   749	    input.history.push({ role: 'assistant', content: `deterministic:${buildToolHistoryLabel(action)}` });
   750	    input.history.push({ role: 'tool', tool: action.tool, output: sanitizeToolContext(output) });
   751	    break;
   752	  }
   753	
   754	  return deterministicStep;
   755	}
   756	
   757	/**
   758	 * Hydrates a temporary sandbox from a stored source bundle and reads a bounded set of files
   759	 * through the same read-only tool path used by review analysis.
   760	 */
   761	export async function readWorkspaceFilesFromSourceBundle(
   762	  env: Env,
   763	  input: {
   764	    sourceBundleKey: string;
   765	    sandboxId: string;
   766	    paths: string[];
   767	    maxFileBytes?: number;
   768	  }
   769	): Promise<ReviewSourceFileReadResult[]> {
   770	  const maxFileBytes = parseIntegerString(env.REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_FILE_BYTES, DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_FILE_BYTES, 1_024, 200_000);
   771	  const effectiveMaxFileBytes =
   772	    typeof input.maxFileBytes === 'number' && Number.isFinite(input.maxFileBytes)
   773	      ? Math.max(1_024, Math.min(maxFileBytes, Math.floor(input.maxFileBytes)))
   774	      : maxFileBytes;
   775	
   776	  if (!env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS && !env.SOURCE_BUNDLES) {
   777	    throw new Error('WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS or SOURCE_BUNDLES binding is required for review context assembly');
   778	  }
   779	  const bundle =
   780	    (env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS ? await env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null) ??
   781	    (env.SOURCE_BUNDLES ? await env.SOURCE_BUNDLES.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null);
   782	  if (!bundle) {
   783	    throw new Error(`Review source bundle not found: ${input.sourceBundleKey}`);
   784	  }
   785	
   786	  const sandbox = await resolveReviewSandbox(env, input.sandboxId);
   787	  try {
   788	    await hydrateReviewSandbox(sandbox, await bundle.arrayBuffer());
   789	
   790	    const policy: ReviewCommandPolicy = {
   791	      commandAllow: [],
   792	      commandDeny: [],
   793	      maxCommandTimeoutMs: MAX_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_MS,
   794	      maxOutputBytes: DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES,
   795	      rootPath: WORKSPACE_ROOT,
   796	    };
   797	
   798	    const uniquePaths = Array.from(new Set(input.paths.map((path) => path.trim()).filter(Boolean)));
   799	    const results: ReviewSourceFileReadResult[] = [];
   800	    for (const path of uniquePaths) {
   801	      const toolResult = await executeReviewTool(
   802	        sandbox,
   803	        { type: 'tool', tool: 'read_file', args: { path, maxBytes: effectiveMaxFileBytes } },
   804	        policy,
   805	        effectiveMaxFileBytes
   806	      );
   807	      const resultRecord = asRecord(toolResult.result);
   808	      const error = typeof resultRecord.error === 'string' ? resultRecord.error : null;
   809	      const content = typeof resultRecord.content === 'string' ? resultRecord.content : null;
   810	      const bytes = typeof resultRecord.bytes === 'number' && Number.isFinite(resultRecord.bytes)
   811	        ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(resultRecord.bytes))
   812	        : 0;
   813	      const truncated = Boolean(resultRecord.truncated);
   814	      results.push({ path, content, bytes, truncated, error });
   815	    }
   816	
   817	    return results;
   818	  } finally {
   819	    if (typeof sandbox.destroy === 'function') {
   820	      try {
   821	        await sandbox.destroy();
   822	      } catch {
   823	        // Best-effort cleanup only.
   824	      }
   825	    }
   826	  }
   827	}
   828	
   829	/**
   830	 * Runs the model-backed review analysis loop against a hydrated deployment snapshot using
   831	 * the read-only review tools and strict structured-output validation.
   832	 */
   833	export async function runWorkspaceDeploymentAgentAnalysis(
   834	  env: Env,
   835	  input: ReviewAgentPromptInput & {
   836	    deploymentSandboxId: string;
   837	    modelOverride?: string;
   838	  }
   839	): Promise<ReviewAgentAnalysisResult | null> {
   840	  const endpoint = (env.AGENT_SDK_URL ?? '').trim();
   841	  const openrouterApiKey=[REDACTED](input.openrouterApiKey) ?? readOptionalString(env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY);
   842	  if (!endpoint && !openrouterApiKey) {
   843	    return null;
   844	  }
   845	
   846	  const model =
   847	    readOptionalString(input.modelOverride) ??
   848	    readOptionalString(env.REVIEW_MODEL) ??
   849	    readOptionalString(env.AGENT_MODEL) ??
   850	    DEFAULT_REVIEW_MODEL;
   851	  const reasoningEffort = parseReviewReasoningEffort(readOptionalString(env.REVIEW_REASONING_EFFORT));
   852	  const authToken = (env.AGENT_SDK_AUTH_TOKEN ?? '').trim() || null;
   853	  let endpointHost: string | null = null;
   854	  let endpointPath: string | null = null;
   855	  try {
   856	    const parsedEndpoint = new URL(endpoint);
   857	    endpointHost = parsedEndpoint.host;
   858	    endpointPath = parsedEndpoint.pathname;
   859	  } catch {
   860	    endpointHost = null;
   861	    endpointPath = null;
   862	  }
   863	
   864	  const maxSteps = parseIntegerString(env.REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_STEPS, DEFAULT_REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_STEPS, 1, MAX_REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_STEPS);
   865	  const maxFileBytes = parseIntegerString(env.REVIEW_AGENT_MAX_FILE_BYTES, DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_FILE_BYTES, 1_024, 200_000);
   866	  if (!env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS && !env.SOURCE_BUNDLES) {
   867	    throw new Error('WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS or SOURCE_BUNDLES binding is required for review analysis');
   868	  }
   869	  const bundle =
   870	    (env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS ? await env.WORKSPACE_ARTIFACTS.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null) ??
   871	    (env.SOURCE_BUNDLES ? await env.SOURCE_BUNDLES.get(input.sourceBundleKey) : null);
   872	  if (!bundle) {
   873	    throw new Error(`Review source bundle not found: ${input.sourceBundleKey}`);
   874	  }
   875	
   876	  const sandbox = await resolveReviewSandbox(env, input.deploymentSandboxId);
   877	  try {
   878	    await hydrateReviewSandbox(sandbox, await bundle.arrayBuffer());
   879	    const { rootListing, diffSnapshot } = await snapshotInitialContext(sandbox, maxFileBytes);
   880	    const prompt = buildReviewAgentPrompt(
   881	      sanitizePromptInput({
   882	        ...input,
   883	        rootListing,
   884	        diffSnapshot,
   885	      })
   886	    );
   887	    if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   888	      await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_prompt_built', {
   889	        reviewContextId: input.reviewContext.id,
   890	        reasoningEffort: reasoningEffort ?? null,
   891	      });
   892	    }
   893	
   894	    const provider = openrouterApiKey
   895	      ? new OpenRouterReviewProvider(openrouterApiKey, validateReviewAgentAction, 'https://nimbus.dayhaysoos.com', 'Nimbus Review Harness')
   896	      : new CloudflareAgentSdkReviewProvider(
   897	          endpoint,
   898	          authToken,
   899	          env.AGENT_ENDPOINT ?? null,
   900	          readOptionalString(input.openrouterApiKey),
   901	          validateReviewAgentAction
   902	        );
   903	    const providerName = openrouterApiKey ? 'openrouter' : 'cloudflare_agents_sdk';
   904	    const policy: ReviewCommandPolicy = {
   905	      commandAllow: [],
   906	      commandDeny: ['git ', 'rm ', 'npm ', 'pnpm ', 'yarn ', 'bun ', 'mkdir ', 'mv ', 'cp ', 'touch '],
   907	      maxCommandTimeoutMs: MAX_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_MS,
   908	      maxOutputBytes: DEFAULT_REVIEW_MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES,
   909	      rootPath: WORKSPACE_ROOT,
   910	    };
   911	
   912	    const history: ReviewAgentHistoryEntry[] = [];
   913	    const usedTools: string[] = [];
   914	    const changedPaths = input.reviewContext.retrieval.changedFiles.map((file) => file.path);
   915	    const changedPathsSet = new Set(changedPaths);
   916	    const integrationSearchQueries = extractIntegrationSearchQueries(input.reviewContext.retrieval.changedFiles);
   917	    const evidence = initializeEvidenceState();
   918	    let finalValidationError: ReviewAgentOutputError | null = null;
   919	    let repairAttempted = false;
   920	    let repairSucceeded = false;
   921	    let firstPassValid = false;
   922	    let validationErrorCount = 0;
   923	    let dedupedExactCount = 0;
   924	    let fallbackApplied = false;
   925	    let fallbackReason: string | null = null;
   926	    let repairAttemptsUsed = 0;
   927	    let forceCompleteNextStep = false;
   928	
   929	    const { deterministicMaxSteps, providerMaxSteps } = computeReviewStepBudgetsForTests(maxSteps);
   930	
   931	    const deterministicExecutedSteps = await runDeterministicEvidenceCollection({
   932	      sandbox,
   933	      policy,
   934	      maxFileBytes,
   935	      authoritativeDiffSnapshot: input.authoritativeDiffSnapshot,
   936	      changedPaths,
   937	      changedFiles: input.reviewContext.retrieval.changedFiles,
   938	      changedPathsSet,
   939	      evidence,
   940	      usedTools,
   941	      history,
   942	      maxDeterministicSteps: deterministicMaxSteps,
   943	      onLifecycleEvent: input.onLifecycleEvent,
   944	    });
   945	    const providerLoopMaxSteps = Math.max(providerMaxSteps, maxSteps - deterministicExecutedSteps);
   946	    const sensitiveChangedPaths = changedPaths.filter((path) => isSensitiveChangedPath(path));
   947	    if (sensitiveChangedPaths.length > 0) {
   948	      history.push({
   949	        role: 'assistant',
   950	        content:
   951	          `analysis_focus: prioritize concrete correctness review in sensitive changed paths before lower-risk neighboring code: ${JSON.stringify(sensitiveChangedPaths)}.`,
   952	      });
   953	    }
   954	
   955	    for (let step = 1; step <= providerLoopMaxSteps; step += 1) {
   956	      if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   957	        await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_provider_request_started', {
   958	          step,
   959	          endpointHost,
   960	          endpointPath,
   961	          hasAuthToken: Boolean(authToken),
   962	          hasOpenrouterApiKey: Boolean(openrouterApiKey),
   963	          provider: providerName,
   964	        });
   965	      }
   966	
   967	      const action = await provider.next({
   968	        prompt,
   969	        model,
   970	        reasoningEffort,
   971	        maxSteps: providerLoopMaxSteps,
   972	        step,
   973	        history,
   974	        forceComplete: forceCompleteNextStep,
   975	      });
   976	      forceCompleteNextStep = false;
   977	
   978	      if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   979	        await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_step_planned', {
   980	          step,
   980	          step,
   981	          type: action.type,
   982	          tool: action.type === 'tool' ? action.tool : null,
   983	        });
   984	      }
   985	
   986	      if (action.type === 'complete') {
   987	        const missingEvidence = collectMissingEvidenceRequirements({
   988	          evidence,
   989	          changedPaths,
   990	          requiresCrossFileEvidence: integrationSearchQueries.length > 0,
   991	        });
   992	        if (missingEvidence.length > 0 && step <= providerLoopMaxSteps) {
   993	          if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
   994	            await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_evidence_insufficient', {
   995	              step,
   996	              missingEvidence,
   997	            });
   998	          }
   999	          history.push({
  1000	            role: 'assistant',
  1001	            content: 'analysis_guard: completion rejected due to insufficient evidence; gather required tool evidence before completing.',
  1002	          });
  1003	          history.push({
  1004	            role: 'tool',
  1005	            tool: 'analysis_guard',
  1006	            output: {
  1007	              ok: false,
  1008	              missingEvidence,
  1009	              changedFiles: changedPaths,
  1010	            },
  1011	          });
  1012	          continue;
  1013	        }
  1014	
  1015	        if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
  1016	          await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_model_output_received', { step, repairAttempted });
  1017	        }
  1018	        try {
  1019	          const parsed = parseCompleteActionPayload(action);
  1020	          const validated = validateOutputOrThrow(parsed);
  1021	          const followUpReview = deriveFollowUpReviewMetadata(parsed, {
  1022	            findings: validated.output.findings,
  1023	            furtherPassesLowYield: validated.output.furtherPassesLowYield,
  1024	          });
  1025	          firstPassValid = repairAttemptsUsed === 0;
  1026	          repairAttempted = repairAttemptsUsed > 0;
  1027	          repairSucceeded = repairAttemptsUsed > 0;
  1028	          dedupedExactCount = validated.dedupedExactCount;
  1029	          if (input.onLifecycleEvent) {
  1030	            await input.onLifecycleEvent('review_analysis_output_validated', {
  1031	              firstPassValid,
  1032	              repairAttempted,
  1033	              repairSucceeded,
  1034	              validationErrorCount,
  1035	              findingCount: validated.output.findings.length,
  1036	              followUpReviewScore: followUpReview.score,
  1037	            });
  1038	          }
  1039	          return {
  1040	            findings: validated.output.findings,
  1041	            summary: validated.output.summary,
  1042	            furtherPassesLowYield: validated.output.furtherPassesLowYield,
  1043	            followUpReviewScore: followUpReview.score,
  1044	            followUpReviewRationale: followUpReview.rationale,
  1045	            intent: null,
  1046	            provider: providerName,
  1047	            model,
  1048	            stepsExecuted: step,
  1049	            usedTools,
  1050	            validation: {
  1051	              firstPassValid,
  1052	              repairAttempted,
  1053	              repairSucceeded,
  1054	              validationErrorCount,
  1055	              dedupedExactCount,
  1056	              fallbackApplied,
  1057	              fallbackReason,
  1058	            },
  1059	          };
  1060	        } catch (error) {
  1061	          if (error instanceof ReviewAgentOutputError && action.summary && isGenericProviderCompletionSummary(action.summary)) {
  1062	            error = new ReviewAgentOutputError(
  1063	              'Review agent returned provider completion text instead of structured JSON output'
  1064	            ).withCode('review_analysis_invalid_output', {
  1065	              errors: [{ path: '$', message: 'Provider completion summary returned instead of required JSON payload.' }],
  1066	            });
  1067	          }
  1068	
  1069	          if (error instanceof ReviewAgentOutputError) {
  1070	            finalValidationError = error;
  1071	            const validationErrors = extractValidationErrors(error);

    73	      ...(input.extraPayload ?? {}),
    74	    },
    75	  });
    76	  throw new QueueRetryError(input.throwMessage ?? 'Review retry requested');
    77	}
    78	
    79	/**
    80	 * Handles the case where a review could not be claimed because another worker may already be running it.
    81	 * Detects stale-running reviews and either schedules a retry or marks them failed.
    82	 */
    83	export async function handleUnclaimedReviewRun(
    84	  env: Env,
    85	  reviewId: string,
    86	  existing: ReviewRunResponse,
    87	  allowRetryScheduling: boolean
    88	): Promise<void> {
    89	  if (existing.status !== 'running') {
    90	    return;
    91	  }
    92	
    93	  const attemptTimeoutMs = parseTimeoutMs(env.ATTEMPT_TIMEOUT_MS, DEFAULT_REVIEW_ATTEMPT_TIMEOUT_MS);
    94	  const staleThresholdMs = attemptTimeoutMs + REVIEW_STALE_GRACE_MS;
    95	  const startedAtMs = toTimestampMs(existing.startedAt) ?? toTimestampMs(existing.updatedAt) ?? toTimestampMs(existing.createdAt);
    96	  const staleForMs = startedAtMs === null ? null : Date.now() - startedAtMs;
    97	  const isStale = typeof staleForMs === 'number' && staleForMs >= staleThresholdMs;
    98	  if (!isStale) {
    99	    throw new QueueRetryError('Review run is already running; defer redelivery');
   100	  }
   101	
   102	  const staleForSeconds = Math.floor((staleForMs ?? 0) / 1000);
   103	  const message = `Review execution stalled in running state for ${staleForSeconds}s (timeout threshold ${Math.floor(staleThresholdMs / 1000)}s).`;
   104	  const attemptCount = existing.attemptCount ?? 0;
   105	  if (allowRetryScheduling && attemptCount <= REVIEW_MAX_RETRIES) {
   106	    await scheduleReviewRetry(env, reviewId, {
   107	      attemptCount,
   108	      message,
   109	      reason: 'stale_running_timeout',
   110	      extraPayload: { staleForSeconds },
   111	      throwMessage: 'Review stale-running recovery requested',
   112	    });
   113	  }
   114	
   115	  await replaceReviewFindings(env.DB, reviewId, []);
   116	  await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'failed', {
   117	    report: null,
   118	    markdownSummary: null,
   119	    errorCode: 'review_execution_timeout',
   120	    errorMessage: message,
   121	  });
   122	  await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
   123	    reviewId,
   124	    eventType: 'review_failed',
   125	    payload: {
   126	      code: 'review_execution_timeout',
   127	      message,
   128	    },
   129	  });
   130	}
   131	
   132	/**
   133	 * Final fallback when repeated inline retries leave the review stuck in queued/retry-scheduled state.
   134	 */
   135	export async function finalizeInlineRetryExhaustion(env: Env, reviewId: string): Promise<void> {
   136	  const latest = await getReviewRun(env.DB, reviewId);
   137	  if (latest?.status === 'queued' && latest.error?.code === 'retry_scheduled') {
   138	    const message = `Review ${reviewId} remained queued after inline retries`;
   139	    await replaceReviewFindings(env.DB, reviewId, []);
   140	    await updateReviewRunStatus(env.DB, reviewId, 'failed', {
   141	      report: null,
   142	      markdownSummary: null,
   143	      errorCode: 'review_execution_failed',
   144	      errorMessage: message,
   145	    });
   146	    await appendReviewEvent(env.DB, {
   147	      reviewId,
   148	      eventType: 'review_failed',
   149	      payload: {
   150	        code: 'review_execution_failed',
   151	        message,
   152	      },
   153	    });
   154	  }
   155	}
   156	
   157	export function shouldRetryReviewError(error: unknown): boolean {
   158	  if (error instanceof QueueRetryError) {
   159	    return true;
   160	  }
   161	  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
   162	  return transientReviewFailure(message);
   163	}
